# REQ-TA-FR-001 只读分析（Understand → Plan → Stop）

仓库：`[repo-root]`（WSL 下访问 `[local path]`）
分支/提交：`integration` @ `03e7d48796725fb1800fac9b6ee6726690134e27`（=`03e7d48`，即 PR #11 合并提交），工作区干净。**与所给改前测试记录完全同一提交**，因此 Windows/integration @ 03e7d48 的 1407 passed / 6 skipped / 1 deselected / 24 warnings / 100 subtests / 48.53s 可直接作为本提交的基线记录，无需区分提交差异。
需求状态：`REQ-TA-FR-001` 在 `docs/req/requirements.yaml:41-52`，`priority: must`，`status: implement #WuYu`（`#WuYu` 是行尾注释，schema 值仍为 `implement`）。这是本任务唯一正式依据。候选文档中的三档值仅为示例（`requirement-candidates.md:128-143` 明确标注 illustrative）。

---

## 1. 需求与现状

### 1.1 FR-001 重述（我的理解）
在分析一只股票之前，用户应能选择 `quick` / `standard` / `deep` 三档分析模式之一，该模式决定四件事：①分析员名单 ②辩论轮数预算 ③风控讨论轮数预算 ④每位分析员的工具调用轮数上限。验收要点（来自 `fit_criterion`，`requirements.yaml:44`）：
- 合法模式必须“精确”套用批准的四项映射；
- 非法模式必须在**任何 LLM 调用之前**被拒绝，错误信息列出合法值；
- 不设置模式时**完全复现 v0.7.1 默认**；
- 同时给出模式与显式设置时，**显式设置只按 key 覆盖对应字段**（逐项覆盖，规则由 ADR 固定并测试）；
- 报告元数据同时记录 **requested** 与 **resolved** 两项。

### 1.2 四项设置：在哪里“设置”、在哪里“实际生效”

| 设置 | 默认值/定义位置 | 图入口生效位置 | 实际约束点 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 分析员名单 `selected_analysts` | 图入口默认 `("market","social","news","fundamentals")` `trading_graph.py:56`；`setup_graph` 默认同值 `setup.py:110` | 构造函数参数 → `set()` 后 `self.selected_analysts` `trading_graph.py:109`；传给 `setup_graph` `trading_graph.py:112` | `build_analyst_execution_plan` 只允许 `market/social/news/fundamentals` `analyst_execution.py:38-55`；节点装配 `setup.py:144-146` |
| 辩论轮数 `max_debate_rounds` | 默认 `1` `default_config.py:133`；env `TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_DEBATE_ROUNDS` `default_config.py:20` | 传入 `ConditionalLogic` `trading_graph.py:84-87` | `should_continue_debate`：`count >= 2*max_debate_rounds` 时结束 `conditional_logic.py:15-18` |
| 风控讨论轮数 `max_risk_discuss_rounds` | 默认 `1` `default_config.py:134`；env `TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_RISK_ROUNDS` `default_config.py:21` | 同上 `trading_graph.py:86` | `should_continue_risk_analysis`：`count >= 3*max_risk_discuss_rounds` 时结束 `conditional_logic.py:25-28` |
| 工具调用轮数上限 `max_tool_rounds` | 默认 `20` `default_config.py:137`；env `TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS` `default_config.py:22` | 读取并校验 `2*max_tool_rounds+2 < max_recur_limit(100)`，否则抛错 `trading_graph.py:91-95`；传入 `GraphSetup` `trading_graph.py:96-101` | 每个分析员私有图：`rounds() >= max_tool_rounds` → 进入 `wrap_up` `setup.py:73-83`，`wrap_up` 后 `END` `setup.py:89` |

补充事实：
- `max_recur_limit=100`（`default_config.py:135`）。因约束 `2*max_tool_rounds+2 < 100`，`max_tool_rounds` **有效上限为 48**（49 会触发 `trading_graph.py:92` 的抛错）。这条对“deep 档工具上限”的取值至关重要。
- 环境变量覆盖是“导入时”折叠进 `DEFAULT_CONFIG`（`default_config.py:74-83,209`），空串视为未设置（`default_config.py:68`），非法 int/bool 在导入期抛 `ValueError`（`default_config.py:54-61,70-73`）。
- `run_settings()` 允许列表当前记录 `analysts / max_debate_rounds / max_risk_discuss_rounds` 等，但**不含 `max_tool_rounds`、也不含 profile**（`trading_graph.py:255-275`）。`_log_state` 写入 `run_settings`（`trading_graph.py:465`）；`save_reports` 把 `run_settings()` 传给报告头（`trading_graph.py:286`）；报告头消费字段 `reporting.py:28-43`、HTML `report_html.py:224-246`。即：工具上限与 requested/resolved 目前都**没有**进入报告——这是 FR-001 需要补的缺口。

### 1.3 交互式 CLI vs Python 入口的默认行为（关键区分）

- **交互式 CLI**（`tradingagents` 裸命令）：`main.analyze` → `run_analysis`（`main.py:91-93`、`run.py:99-113`）→ `get_user_selections` 逐步提问。分析员由 **Step 4 checkbox** 选择，默认**不预选**（`prompts.py:161-193`，`checked=value.value in (default or [])`，无 prefs 时全空）；“研究深度”Step 5 映射到**辩论与风控两个轮数**（同一整数），选项为 1/3/5（`prompts.py:199-203`），再由 `_build_run_config` 写回 `max_debate_rounds` 与 `max_risk_discuss_rounds`（`run.py:69-81`）。**CLI 没有任何工具轮数或模式的输入项**；`max_tool_rounds` 只能来自默认/env。`depth_from_env()` 在两轮数都被 env 设置时跳过深度问题（`selections.py:52-55,236-242`）。无 TTY 时先列出所有缺口再退出（`run.py:103-109`，`selections.py:58-71`）。
- **Python 入口**（`main.py` 和直接构造 `TradingAgentsGraph`）：`main.py` 用 `DEFAULT_CONFIG.copy()` 构造图，分析员用**构造默认**（四项全开），不提问；轮数取 `DEFAULT_CONFIG`。`backtest` 子命令也是 Python 路径，`--analysts` 省略时“资产类型允许的全部”（`main.py:132-133`）。
- 结论：所谓“v0.7.1 默认”存在**两条**语义——Python/图入口的四分析员 + 轮数 1/1/20；交互 CLI 的分析员由用户勾选、轮数由深度 1/3/5。FR-001 的“省略模式复现 v0.7.1”必须以**入口为作用域**分别定义，不可混为一谈（见歧义 Q1）。

### 1.4 需要人工确认的歧义
- **Q1 作用域**：“省略模式复现 v0.7.1”是针对图/Python 入口（四项全开、1/1/20），还是也约束交互 CLI（分析员不改、深度问题保留）？建议：省略模式**完全不注入**，各入口行为保持现状；“复现”以入口为单位断言。
- **Q2 显式设置识别**：`max_debate_rounds` 与 `max_risk_discuss_rounds` 在交互 CLI 中由同一个“深度”整数驱动（`run.py:69-81`）。当模式与显式深度同时存在时，“显式”到底指数值相等即算显式，还是指数值“与当前默认不同”？这会影响逐项覆盖语义。
- **Q3 分析员是否可被逐项覆盖**：`--analysts` 是 CLI flag（`main.py:58-60`），属于“显式设置”。模式给出名单但用户又传 `--analysts` 时，是否用 `--analysts` 覆盖模式的名单（逐项覆盖适用于名单这一 key）？
- **Q4 低值支持**：默认辩论/风控已是 1。问题问“更低是否受支持”——代码层面 `max_debate_rounds=0` 会导致 `count>=0` 立即结束（`conditional_logic.py:15-16`），即 0 轮辩论、直接进 Research Manager；风控 0 同理直接进 PM。这在语法上可行，但语义上是“跳过辩论”，是否允许 `quick` 使用 0 需团队拍板（见 Q4/建议表）。
- **Q5 max_tool_rounds 进报告**：FR-001 要求记录 requested/resolved，但 `run_settings` 未含 `max_tool_rounds`；是否本次一并加入允许列表（会扩大 GOV-003/QR-004 的允许列表，需与那两位负责人协调）。
- **Q6 报告字段载体**：requested/resolved 放在 `run_settings()` 扩展字段（影响报告头 + `full_states_log`），还是放独立元数据块？影响 FR-008/GOV-003 的兼容。

---

## 2. 三档配置建议与 ADR 草案

### 2.1 建议映射表（全部“待团队确认”）

| 模式 | 分析员名单 | 辩论上限 | 风控讨论上限 | 每位分析员工具轮上限 |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| `quick` | 待团队确认（建议 `market,news` 或 `market,social,news`） | 待团队确认（建议 1） | 待团队确认（建议 1） | 待团队确认（建议 3） |
| `standard` | 待团队确认（建议四项全开 = 现默认） | 待团队确认（建议 1 = 现默认） | 待团队确认（建议 1 = 现默认） | 待团队确认（建议 20 = 现默认） |
| `deep` | 待团队确认（建议四项全开） | 待团队确认（建议 3） | 待团队确认（建议 3） | 待团队确认（建议 20，不得 >48） |

**取舍说明**
- `quick`：目的对应 COST-001/PERF-001 的“削 token/降时延”。建议减少分析员 + 降低工具轮上限；由于辩论/风控默认已是 1，**不建议再降到 0**（0 等于完全跳过辩论，语义变化过大，且可能与 INV-004“不得静默省略贡献”冲突需评估）。若团队确实想要更低成本，可评估 0，但需在 ADR 明确“0 = 跳过该段”。
- `standard`：建议**等于当前默认**（四项全开、1/1/20），这样 `standard` 与“省略模式”一致，兼容与心智负担最低。
- `deep`：提高辩论/风控到 3（对齐 CLI 已有“Medium=3”语义，`prompts.py:201`），工具轮可维持 20。**硬约束：工具轮必须 ≤48**（否则 `trading_graph.py:92` 直接抛错）。

**可行性校验（依据现有代码）**
- 低值：辩论/风控 `>=0` 均被 `conditional_logic.py` 接受；默认 1 已是低值，**“更低”= 0 在语法可行但语义为跳过**（Q4）。
- 工具轮上限：受 `2*max_tool_rounds+2 < 100` 约束，最大值 48（`trading_graph.py:91-95`）。`deep` 若想 >48 必须同时上调 `max_recur_limit`，属另一项设计决策。
- 分析员子集：`build_analyst_execution_plan` 接受任意非空子集，`quick` 用子集可行（`analyst_execution.py:38-55`）。
- 结论：建议值（1/1/3、1/1/20、3/3/20 及子集）**在当前代码下均可行**。

### 2.2 设计驱动因素（ADD 视角）
1. **兼容优先（SC-2）**：不得静默改变 v0.7.1 行为；省略模式=零注入。
2. **可测/确定性**：映射必须在 LLM 调用前完成并可被离线断言。
3. **单一事实源**：四项配置现已分散在 `default_config` 与图构造；模式需一个明确解析点，避免多处漂移。
4. **与既有 env/CLI 优先级共存**：现有规则是“env 显式覆盖 > 交互选择”（`run.py:62-81`、`selections.py:52-55`），新模式必须不破坏它（Q2）。
5. **可观测**：requested/resolved 可追踪（对接 QR-004/GOV-003）。
6. **最小侵入**：不新增运行时依赖（SC-5），不触碰冻结区。

### 2.3 配置解析位置：两种方案比较

**方案 A：在 `default_config.py` 解析（模式作为 config key + env `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE`）**
- 做法：加 `profile` 键；`build_default_config()` 先取 env `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE`（或用 default profile），把四项“模式默认”折叠进 config；随后再应用**显式** env 覆盖（现有 `_ENV_OVERRIDES`），天然实现“显式逐项覆盖”。校验在 `build_default_config` 内完成。
- 优点：与现有 env 折叠机制一致（`default_config.py:64-74`）；Python 入口与 CLI 都从 `DEFAULT_CONFIG` 起步，天然共享；requested 可在 config 中留痕供 `run_settings` 读取。
- 缺点：`DEFAULT_CONFIG` 是**导入期单例**（`default_config.py:209`），运行中切换模式不可行；若把模式默认写进 DEFAULT_CONFIG 再写“显式值”，需注意“导入时”与“每 run”两次解析的时序（CLI `_build_run_config` 会再次覆盖，`run.py:59-96`）。
- 影响：需与 `cli/run.py:_build_run_config`、`selections.depth_from_env` 协调，避免深度问题与模式冲突。

**方案 B：在 `graph/trading_graph.py` 构造期解析（模式作为构造参数/函数）**
- 做法：新增 `resolve_profile(profile, explicit_overrides)` 纯函数，`TradingAgentsGraph.__init__` 调用它得到四项最终值，再建图。
- 优点：**每次 run 解析**，无导入期单例问题；作用点紧贴四项实际生效处（`conditional_logic.py`、`setup.py`）；非法模式可在构造期（= 第一次 LLM 调用前）抛错；纯函数极易离线单测。
- 缺点：需要决定 requested/profile 如何回传（构造参数 vs config），并让 CLI 也走同一函数（否则 CLI 仍按深度提问）。

**推荐：B（图构造期解析）+ A 的一小部分（`profile` 作为 `DEFAULT_CONFIG` 的可选键与 `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE` env 入手，但不把模式值预烘进四项）**。理由：解析点唯一且贴近生效点、可离线测、避免导入期单例陷阱、对 Python 与 CLI 都可用；`profile` 键仅作“请求输入”的来源，真正的四项映射由 `resolve_profile` 在每次 run 计算。

**逐项覆盖规则（必须遵守正式需求）**
- 输入分两类：`profile`（可有可无）+ 一组**显式设置**（`analysts`、`max_debate_rounds`、`max_risk_discuss_rounds`、`max_tool_rounds`）。
- 算法：`resolved = profile_mapping[profile]` 的四项为底；对每个**显式给出的 key**，用其值逐项覆盖；未显式给出的 key 取模式值。
- **如何识别“显式”**：以“该 key 是否由用户/上层显式提供”为准，而非“是否等于默认”。建议实现上用“显式来源集合”判断：env `TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_DEBATE_ROUNDS/_MAX_RISK_ROUNDS/_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS` 被设置、CLI 传了 `--analysts`、或 API 调用方显式传了对应 config 键。→ **此点依赖 Q2/Q3 拍板**（尤其 CLI 的深度整数同时驱动两个轮数）。
- **完全省略模式**：`profile is None` 时不调用映射，`resolved` 直接等于现有默认/env 折叠值 → 复刻 v0.7.1。**Requires Q1 确认入口作用域**。
- 未知 key/非法模式：在解析期抛 `ValueError`，消息含合法值 `quick, standard, deep`（对接“LLM 调用前拒绝”）。

### 2.4 ADR 草案（骨架，ID 待定，暂记 ADR-TBD-FR001）
- 状态：Proposed（待团队批准）
- 背景/问题：四项设置分散、无模式键；需在不破坏 v0.7.1 的前提下引入三档。
- 决策：采用方案 B；模式映射值为上表（待确认）；逐项覆盖（显式 key 覆盖模式值）；`profile=None` 零注入。
- 备选：方案 A（列出拒绝理由：导入期单例、与 CLI 深度耦合）。
- 后果：影响 `default_config.py`（新增可选 `profile` 键 + env）、`graph/trading_graph.py`（解析调用 + `run_settings` 扩展）、`cli/run.py`/`selections.py`（模式入口与深度冲突处理）、`reporting.py`/`report_html.py`（requested/resolved 展示）、新增 `resolve_profile` 纯函数 + 测试。
- 合规：SC-2 扩展现有、SC-3 注释引用 `ADR-TBD-FR001; REQ-TA-FR-001`；不新增依赖（SC-5）。
- **待确认接口/映射**：模式到四项的最终数值；显式识别规则（Q2/Q3）；requested/resolved 字段名与载体（Q6）；是否把 `max_tool_rounds` 加入 `run_settings` 允许列表（Q5）。
- **何时重新评估**：①若团队要 `deep` 工具轮 >48（须改 `max_recur_limit`）；②若 `quick` 采用 0 轮辩论（语义变更）；③若 FR-008/GOV-003 的允许列表定稿方式与本 ADR 冲突；④若架构蓝图（当前 `docs/architecture/` **不存在**）给出不同的 element→module 归属（AGENTS.md §5、SC-1）。

## 3. 实施计划（FR-001 范围）

### 3.1 需求→步骤→文件 追踪
| 需求点 | 步骤 | 文件（拟改/拟增） |
|---|---|---|
| 定义三档映射值 | S1 加常量映射 + `resolve_profile` 纯函数 | 增 `src/tradingagents/profiles.py`（或 `default_config.py` 内，待定）；改 `default_config.py`（可选 `profile` 键 + `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE` env 行） |
| 合法模式精确生效（四项） | S2 在构造期解析并传入图 | 改 `graph/trading_graph.py`（`__init__` 调用 `resolve_profile`，构造 `ConditionalLogic`/`GraphSetup` 用解析后值） |
| 非法模式在 LLM 前拒绝并列合法值 | S1/S2 解析期校验 | `profiles.py`/`default_config.py` + 在 `trading_graph.__init__`（LLM 客户端创建前 `trading_graph.py:78-80`）执行 |
| 省略模式=v0.7.1 | S1 的“`profile is None` 零注入”分支 | `profiles.py` |
| 显式逐项覆盖 | S1 显式集合参数 + S4 入口接线 | `profiles.py`、`cli/run.py`（`_build_run_config`）、`cli/selections.py`（深度冲突）、`cli/main.py`（`--profile` flag） |
| 报告记录 requested+resolved | S3 扩展 `run_settings` + 渲染 | `graph/trading_graph.py`（`run_settings`）、`reporting.py`（`_header`）、`report_html.py`（`_run_details`） |
| Python 入口一致性 | S4 让 `main.py`/API 也走 `resolve_profile` | `main.py`/构造默认（若保留默认路径则仅确认零注入） |

### 3.2 跨模块接口（提案，**不擅自实现**；AGENTS.md §6 要求先提案待批）
- `resolve_profile(profile: str | None, *, explicit: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ResolvedProfile` 纯函数，返回 `{analysts: tuple[str,...], max_debate_rounds:int, max_risk_discuss_rounds:int, max_tool_rounds:int}`；未知 profile 抛 `ValueError("unknown profile '<x>'; choose from quick, standard, deep")`。
- `TradingAgentsGraph.__init__` 增可选 `profile` 参数或在 config 中读 `profile`；构造期调用 `resolve_profile`；`self.requested_settings` / `self.resolved_settings` 供 `run_settings()` 读取。
- `run_settings()` 返回体新增 `profile_requested`、`profile_resolved`、`max_tool_rounds`（**加允许列表需 Q5 批准**）。
- CLI：`--profile`（`typer.Option`，取值校验放在解析期）；`_build_run_config` 仅接入“显式集合”，不改变现有 env 优先级。
- 说明：以上签名是我按需求提出的**建议**，是否采用归团队；本会话不落地。

### 3.3 改动范围
- 拟改：`src/tradingagents/default_config.py`、`src/tradingagents/graph/trading_graph.py`、`src/cli/run.py`、`src/cli/selections.py`、`src/cli/main.py`、`src/tradingagents/reporting.py`、`src/tradingagents/report_html.py`。
- 拟增：`src/tradingagents/profiles.py`（解析纯函数，按 SC-1 单一模块）。
- 拟增测试：`test/offline/test_profiles.py`（专项，引用 `REQ-TA-FR-001`）。
- **明确不碰**：`.opencode/rules.md`、`.agent/rules.md`、`.env*`、`LICENSE`、CHANGELOG 已发布段、`.github/**`、`docs/req/requirements.yaml`（除团队要求的状态/追踪更新，且需 schema gate）、`llm_clients/**`、`dataflows/**` 核心安全路径、任何 `test/live/**`。

### 3.4 测试策略（概览，详见第 4 节）
- 每个 fit criterion 至少一条离线测试；解析纯函数用参数化覆盖三档 + 非法 + 省略 + 逐项覆盖。
- 全量离线回归：`pytest -q`（默认 `-m "not integration"`，`pyproject.toml:61`），对照 03e7d48 基线（1407 passed…），**零回归**；`ruff check .` 通过。
- 测试用 `ScriptedModel`（`test/offline/test_graph_end_to_end.py:52-96`）与 `create_tier_client` monkeypatch（`:127`）作替身，**断言 LLM 未被调用**。

### 3.5 可独立验证的实施顺序
1. **S0 基线复跑**（只读）：确认 `pytest -q` 与记录一致（1 步，不改码）。
2. **S1 解析纯函数 + 单测**：`profiles.py` + `test_profiles.py`；仅测试，不需接线即可验证（映射/非法/省略/覆盖）。
3. **S2 图构造接线**：`trading_graph.__init__` 调用；验证三档在 `ConditionalLogic`/`GraphSetup` 生效；验证非法模式在 LLM 客户端创建前抛错（用替身断言零调用）。
4. **S3 报告元数据**：`run_settings` 扩展 + `reporting`/`report_html` 渲染；验证 requested/resolved 落盘（`complete_report.md`、`full_states_log_*.json`）。
5. **S4 CLI/入口接线**：`--profile`、`_build_run_config` 显式集合、深度冲突提示；验证交互与 headless 路径一致、省略模式不变。
6. **S5 全量回归 + lint**：`ruff check .`、`pytest -q`，对照基线，出证据。

**用户请求的模式 → 最终生效配置 → 报告** 的流向：用户请求（CLI `--profile` / env `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE` / API 参数）→ `resolve_profile`（叠加显式 key）→ 构造期四项（喂给 `ConditionalLogic`/`GraphSetup`/`build_analyst_execution_plan`）→ `run_settings()` 记录 requested+resolved → `_log_state` 写 `full_states_log_*.json` 且 `save_reports` 写报告头/HTML。

## 4. 可审批的离线测试计划（可贴到 GitHub test-plan issue）

> Title: `test-plan(FR-001): three analysis profiles (quick/standard/deep)`
> Requirement: `REQ-TA-FR-001` (must, implement). Scope: FR-001 only. All tests deterministic, network-free, under `test/offline/`, each citing `REQ-TA-FR-001`.

| 验收条件 | 测试场景 | 预期结果 |
|---|---|---|
| 三档映射实际生效 | 对 `quick/standard/deep` 各构造图，断言 `conditional_logic.max_debate_rounds`=映射值、`max_risk_discuss_rounds`=映射值、`graph_setup.max_tool_rounds`=映射值；并在替身上跑一次 run 断言分析员节点集合=映射名单 | 四项与批准映射**逐项相等**；`ScriptedModel.calls` 中参与的分析员与名单一致 |
| 省略模式兼容原版 | 不传 profile，分别在（a）图/Python 入口（b）CLI `_build_run_config` 路径 | 四项等于默认/env 折叠值（图：四项全开、1/1/20；CLI：保持现有深度与勾选语义），即 v0.7.1 行为 |
| 非法模式在 LLM 前报错并列合法值 | `profile="turbo"` 构造 `TradingAgentsGraph`（替身 `create_tier_client` 计数） | 抛 `ValueError`，消息含 `quick`、`standard`、`deep`；**`create_tier_client` 调用次数 == 0**、任何模型 `_generate` 调用 == 0 |
| 显式设置逐项覆盖 | 给 profile=`quick` 且显式设置其中 1–2 个 key（如只覆盖 `max_debate_rounds`，或只覆盖 `analysts`） | 被显式设置的 key=显式值，其余 key=quick 值；参数化覆盖“只覆盖一个”“覆盖多个”“覆盖全部” |
| 报告同时记录请求值与实际值 | 有覆盖项的 run 后读取 `complete_report.md` 头、`complete_report.html`、`full_states_log_*.json` | 三处均含 requested（profile 与显式原值）与 resolved（四项最终值），且二者不同处正确反映覆盖 |
| 未调用 LLM（替身验证） | `ScriptedModel` + monkeypatch `create_tier_client`（`test_graph_end_to_end.py:52,127`） | 非法/提前拒绝场景中模型调用计数为 0；合法场景调用计数>0 以证明替身有效 |
| 全量离线回归 | `pytest -q`（`-m "not integration"`）、`ruff check .` | 无回归；对照 03e7d48 基线（1407 passed/6 skipped/1 deselected/24 warnings/100 subtests）说明差异；`ruff` 通过 |
| 低值边界（Q4 相关，条件性） | 若团队批准 0 轮：断言辩论/风控 0 → 直接进 Manager/PM；若批准工具轮=48 上限边界 | 边界取值通过；`max_tool_rounds=49` 仍按 `trading_graph.py:92` 抛错（负向测试） |
| 注释/追踪合规 | 扫描新增源文件 docstring/函数注释 | 含 `ADR-TBD-FR001` 与 `REQ-TA-FR-001`（SC-3） |

**如何用替身证明“未调用 LLM”**：文件级 monkeypatch `trading_graph.create_tier_client` 返回带计数器的 `_Client`/`ScriptedModel`，并让 `ScriptedModel._generate` 记录调用；非法模式测试在构造期即抛错，断言计数器为 0。
**如何做全量离线回归**：从干净环境 `pytest -q`（默认排除 integration）+ `ruff check .`，与 03e7d48 记录对比；任何新增/减少的通过数说明原因；不运行 `test/live/**`、不调用真实 API。
**门禁**：先开本 issue 并获 `test-approved` 标签后再进入 S2+（对照 `verification-constraints.md` VC-4 gate 1）。

## 0. 本轮对上一轮两处结论的修正

**修正 1（0 轮的解释）**：上一轮说“`max_debate_rounds=0` 会立即结束、直接进 Research Manager”**不准确**。图里 `START→Bull Researcher`、`Trader→Aggressive Analyst` 是**无条件边**（`setup.py:166,176`），路由判断只在第一位研究员/风控分析员跑完之后才发生（`conditional_logic.py:12-21,23-33`）。所以轮数=0 的真实语义是“**第一位（Bull / Aggressive）仍执行一轮，随后直接切换**”，而非“零次发言”。据此：
- 轮数的下限受“至少一位发言人”硬约束，`quick` 用 1/1 是**最自然的低值**，不必也不建议引入 0；本轮三档都用 `≥1`，此修正只用于防止未来误设 0 时对语义的误解。
- 若将来评估 0，必须在 ADR 明确“0 = 第一位仍发言一次后立即结束”，并加边界测试；本轮不采用。

**修正 2（CLI 解析时机）**：`run_analysis` 的顺序是：无终端缺口检查（`run.py:103-109`）→ `get_user_selections` 全流程提问（`run.py:111`）→ `_build_run_config`（`run.py:113`）→ 构造 `TradingAgentsGraph`（创建 LLM 客户端，`run.py:123-128`）→ `message_buffer.init_for_analysis` 进度显示（`run.py:130`）。要满足“非法模式在任何 LLM 调用前拒绝、且在提问与进度显示前拿到解析结果”，解析必须发生在 **`run.py:111` 之前/之内**（即 `--profile`/`TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE` 在进入交互提问前先校验），并让模式解析结果驱动后续提问的默认与进度显示的名单。

---

## 1. 最终三档映射（已定）

三个数字依次为 多空辩论 / 风控讨论 / 每位分析员工具调用上限。

| 模式 | 分析员名单 | 辩论 | 风控 | 工具轮 |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| `quick` | market, news | 1 | 1 | 3 |
| `standard` | market, social, news, fundamentals | 1 | 1 | 20 |
| `deep` | market, social, news, fundamentals | 3 | 3 | 20 |

可行性核对（依据现有代码）：名单子集与非空校验由 `analyst_execution.py:38-55` 支持；`social` 键存在（`analyst_execution.py` 的 `ANALYST_NODE_SPECS`、`cli/models.py:4-10`）；工具轮 3/20 满足 `2*max_tool_rounds+2 < 100`（`trading_graph.py:91-95`）；轮数 1/1/3/3 由 `conditional_logic.py` 支持。**全部可行。**

---

## 2. ADR 草案（修订版）

ADR-ID：`ADR-TBD-FR001`（编号待团队分配）
状态：Proposed（待审批）
Implements: `REQ-TA-FR-001`（+ 依赖 `REQ-TA-INV-003` 工具轮上限）

### 2.1 背景
四项设置（分析员、辩论、风控、工具轮）现分散于 `default_config.py` 与图构造；无模式键。需在不破坏 v0.7.1 行为、且保留现有“env 显式 > 交互选择”优先级（`run.py:59-81`、`selections.py:52-55`）的前提下引入三档。

### 2.2 决策
1. **映射值**：采用第 1 节表（已定）。
2. **解析位置**：`graph/trading_graph.py` 构造期通过纯函数 `resolve_profile(...)` 解析（方案 B），并在 `default_config.py` 增加可选 `profile` 键与 `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE` env 行作为请求来源；模式值**不预烘进四项默认**。理由：解析点贴近生效点、每次 run 解析（避开导入期单例 `default_config.py:209`）、可离线单测、Python 与 CLI 共用。
3. **逐项覆盖（按输入来源）**：`resolved = mapping[profile]` 为底；对每个“**由输入显式提供**”的 key，用其值覆盖；未显式提供的 key 取模式值。识别“显式”=看来源，不看是否等于默认：`TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_DEBATE_ROUNDS`/`_MAX_RISK_ROUNDS`/`_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS` 被设置、CLI 传了 `--analysts`、或 API 调用方显式传键。示例：`deep` + 显式辩论=1 → 辩论 1，**风控仍 3**、工具轮仍 20。
4. **省略模式**：`profile is None` → 不调用映射，`resolved` 等于原有默认/env 折叠值；**各入口按原有流程执行**（图/Python：四项全开、1/1/20；交互 CLI：仍走原有分析员勾选与深度提问，不注入模式）。
5. **报告元数据**：记录三项——**请求的模式**、**显式覆盖项（key→值）**、**实际生效的四项**。
6. **非法模式**：解析期抛 `ValueError`，消息列出 `quick, standard, deep`；发生在任何 LLM 客户端创建之前。

### 2.3 0 轮语义（记录，非本轮采用）
依据修正 1：图先执行第一位 Bull/Aggressive，再判断。0 轮=第一位仍发言一次后立即切换；本轮三档均 ≥1，不使用 0。

### 2.4 备选与后果
- 备选方案 A（在 `default_config.py` 预烘）：拒绝，因导入期单例 + 与 CLI 深度提问耦合（详见上轮）。
- 影响文件：`default_config.py`、`graph/trading_graph.py`、`reporting.py`、`report_html.py`、`cli/run.py`、`cli/selections.py`、`cli/main.py`；新增 `profiles.py`（`resolve_profile`，按 SC-1 单一模块——归属待架构蓝图确认，`docs/architecture/` 当前不存在）。
- 合规：SC-2 扩展不替换；SC-3 注释引用 `ADR-TBD-FR001; REQ-TA-FR-001`；SC-5 不新增依赖。
- 何时重新评估：①工具轮需 >48（涉 `max_recur_limit`）；②任何档引入 0 轮；③`resolve_profile` 的 element→module 归属与蓝图冲突；④与 FR-008/GOV-003 的 `run_settings` 允许列表定稿冲突。

### 2.5 待确认接口
- `resolve_profile(profile: str|None, *, explicit: Mapping[str,Any]) -> ResolvedProfile`（四项 + requested + applied_overrides）。
- `run_settings()` 扩展字段名（`profile_requested` / `profile_overrides` / `max_tool_rounds` 等）——是否纳入允许列表需与 GOV-003/QR-004/FR-008 负责人对齐。

## 3. 实施计划（修订版，FR-001 范围）

### 3.1 追踪
| 需求点 | 步骤 | 文件 |
|---|---|---|
| 三档映射/非法校验/省略零注入 | S1 | 增 `src/tradingagents/profiles.py`；改 `default_config.py`（`profile` 键 + `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE`） |
| 四项在图构造期生效（LLM 前校验） | S2 | 改 `graph/trading_graph.py`（`__init__` 调 `resolve_profile`，在建客户端 `:78-80` **之前**校验） |
| 报告记录 requested/overrides/resolved | S3 | 改 `graph/trading_graph.py`（`run_settings`）、`reporting.py`、`report_html.py` |
| CLI 在提问/进度前解析、与 Python 共用 | S4 | 改 `cli/main.py`（`--profile`）、`cli/selections.py`、`cli/run.py`（解析置于 `run.py:111` 前） |
| Python 入口共用同一规则 | S4 | `main.py` / 构造默认路径确认走 `resolve_profile`（省略=零注入） |

### 3.2 跨模块接口（提案，待批）
- `resolve_profile(...)` 纯函数，返回 `analysts/max_debate_rounds/max_risk_discuss_rounds/max_tool_rounds` + `requested_profile` + `applied_overrides`。
- 图构造：`TradingAgentsGraph(..., profile=None)` 或读 `config["profile"]`；`self.requested_settings/self.resolved_settings` 供 `run_settings()`。
- CLI：`--profile`（取值校验在解析期）；`run_analysis` 在 `get_user_selections` 前完成解析，用解析结果：(a) 作为分析员提问的预选默认，(b) 作为 `message_buffer.init_for_analysis` 的名单，(c) 传入 `_build_run_config` 作为底值；`--analysts` 等显式来源逐项覆盖。
- 说明：签名是建议，是否采用归团队；本轮不落地。

### 3.3 CLI 解析时机（本次明确）
`run_analysis` 新顺序：无终端缺口检查（`run.py:103-109`）→ **解析 profile（`--profile`/`TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE`，非法即退出并列合法值，`run.py:111` 之前）** → `get_user_selections`（用解析名单作预选/默认，`run.py:111`）→ `_build_run_config`（模式底值 + 显式逐项覆盖，`run.py:113`）→ 构造图（`run.py:123`）→ 进度显示（用解析名单，`run.py:130`）。这样非法模式在**任何提问与 LLM 客户端创建前**被拦下，且进度显示用的是解析后的名单。

### 3.4 改动范围
- 拟改：`default_config.py`、`graph/trading_graph.py`、`reporting.py`、`report_html.py`、`cli/run.py`、`cli/selections.py`、`cli/main.py`。
- 拟增：`src/tradingagents/profiles.py`、`test/offline/test_profiles.py`。
- **不碰**：`.opencode/rules.md`、`.agent/rules.md`、`.env*`、`LICENSE`、CHANGELOG 已发布段、`.github/**`、`llm_clients/**`、`dataflows/**` 安全路径、`test/live/**`；`docs/req/requirements.yaml` 仅在团队要求时更新且需过 schema gate。

### 3.5 测试策略与顺序（可独立验证）
- **S0** 复跑基线：`pytest -q` 对照 03e7d48（1407 passed/6 skipped/1 deselected/24 warnings/100 subtests）确认一致。
- **S1** 纯函数 + 单测（不需接线即可验证）：三档映射、非法、省略、逐项覆盖（含 `deep`+辩论=1→风控仍 3）。
- **S2** 图构造接线：断言 `ConditionalLogic`/`GraphSetup` 取到解析值；非法模式在建客户端前抛错（替身计数 0）。
- **S3** 报告：`complete_report.md`/`.html`/`full_states_log_*.json` 含 requested/overrides/resolved。
- **S4** CLI/入口：`--profile` 在提问前解析；交互与 headless 一致；省略模式各入口不变。
- **S5** `ruff check .` + 全量 `pytest -q` 零回归，出证据。

用户请求 → 解析（`resolve_profile` 叠加显式来源）→ 构造期四项（喂 `ConditionalLogic`/`GraphSetup`/`build_analyst_execution_plan`）→ `run_settings()`（requested/overrides/resolved）→ `_log_state` 写 `full_states_log_*.json` 且 `save_reports` 写报告头/HTML。

# REQ-TA-FR-001 修订稿（只读）— 方案 + Issue 正文

仓库 `tradingagents-team-version`，`integration` @ `03e7d48`，工作区干净。三档参数沿用：quick={market,news,1,1,3}；standard={market,social,news,fundamentals,1,1,20}；deep={market,social,news,fundamentals,3,3,20}（顺序：分析员、辩论、风控、工具轮）。

---

## A. ADR 草案（修订版）

**ADR-ID:** `ADR-TBD-FR001`（正式编号待分配） · **状态:** Proposed · **Implements:** `REQ-TA-FR-001`（关联 `REQ-TA-INV-003`）

### A.1 背景
四项设置现分散于 `default_config.py`（`max_debate_rounds=1/`max_risk_discuss_rounds=1`/`max_tool_rounds=20`，`default_config.py:133-137`）与图构造；无模式键。需在不破坏 v0.7.1、且保留“env 显式 > 交互选择”优先级（`run.py:59-81`、`selections.py:52-55`）下引入三档。

### A.2 决策
1. **映射值**：采用上表（已定）。
2. **解析位置**：`graph/trading_graph.py` 构造期通过纯函数 `resolve_profile(...)` 解析；`default_config.py` 增加可选 `profile` 键与 `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE` env 行作为**请求来源**；模式值不预烘进四项默认。
3. **逐项覆盖（按来源）**：`resolved = mapping[profile]` 为底，对每个“**由输入显式提供**”的 key 覆盖，其余取模式值。示例：`deep` + 显式辩论=1 → 辩论 1、**风控仍 3**、工具轮仍 20。
4. **省略模式**：`profile is None` → 不调用映射；各入口**按原有流程执行**（图/Python：四项全开、1/1/20；交互 CLI：沿用分析员勾选与深度提问，不注入）。
5. **报告元数据**：记录 requested 模式、显式覆盖项（key→值）、resolved 四项（字段形状见 A.5）。
6. **非法模式**：解析期抛 `ValueError`，列出 `quick, standard, deep`；早于任何 LLM 客户端创建。
7. **0 轮语义（记录）**：图先执行第一位 Bull/Aggressive 再加路由判断（`setup.py:166,176`、`conditional_logic.py:12-33`），故 0 轮=第一位仍发言一次后切换；本轮三档均 ≥1，不使用 0。

### A.3 分界：显式覆盖 vs DEFAULT_CONFIG 默认值
关键：**DEFAULT_CONFIG 已经折叠了 env**（`default_config.py:74-83,209`），因此“config 里的值”无法区分“默认”与“env 显式”。ADR 采用“按来源判定”，由解析函数显式接收来源集合，而非比对数值是否等于默认：
- **显式来源**（进入 overlay）：CLI `--profile`/`--analysts`、`TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE`、`TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_DEBATE_ROUNDS`、`TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_RISK_ROUNDS`、`TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS`、Python 调用方显式传入的 `explicit_overrides` 键。
- **默认来源**（不进入 overlay）：`DEFAULT_CONFIG` 的初始内置值、或这些 env 未设置时的回退值。
- 实现上 `resolve_profile` 只接收“显式”的映射，DEFAULT_CONFIG 仅作“省略模式”的底值来源；二者在入参结构上分离，不做数值相等比较。

### A.4 checkpoint 签名
- `_run_signature` 已把 `selected_analysts`、`max_debate_rounds`、`max_risk_discuss_rounds` 显式拼入，并对“其余 config（排除 `_NOT_IN_SIGNATURE`）”做 sha256（`trading_graph.py:150-165`）。因此新增的 `profile` 键会自动计入 `settings` 摘要；`max_tool_rounds` 本就在 config 内，也计入摘要。
- **省略模式保留原签名**：不新增任何 config 键值（不写 `profile`，亦不改四项）→ `settings` 摘要与 v0.7.1 逐字节一致 → checkpoint 线程 ID 不变，旧 checkpoint 可继续 resume。
- **改变时区分**：模式改了（四项或 `profile` 不同）→ 摘要/analysts/debate/risk 段不同 → 新线程 ID，重新开始，不复用旧 checkpoint。为可读性，建议把 `profile` 也显式拼入签名串（如 `f"profile={cfg.get('profile')}"`），但**仅在非 None 时追加**以保持省略模式签名不变（待 ADR 确认；若担心 `settings` 摘要已覆盖则可不加）。
- 注意：省略模式必须保证 `resolve_profile` **不把 profile 默认值写入 config**，否则摘要漂移，破坏 A.4 的“签名不变”。

### A.5 报告字段形状（提案，待确认落点）
`run_settings()` 扩展三块（或独立元数据块）：
```
profile_requested: "quick" | "standard" | "deep" | null
profile_overrides: { "<key>": <value>, ... }        # 仅显式覆盖项，空字典表示无覆盖
profile_resolved: {
    "analysts": ["market", "news"],
    "max_debate_rounds": 1,
    "max_risk_discuss_rounds": 1,
    "max_tool_rounds": 3
}
```
同时建议把 `max_tool_rounds` 加入允许列表；渲染到 `reporting.py:_header`、`report_html.py:_run_details`、`full_states_log_*.json`（`trading_graph.py:465`）。是否纳入 `run_settings` 允许列表需与 GOV-003/QR-004/FR-008 负责人对齐。

### A.6 何时重新评估
工具轮需 >48（涉 `max_recur_limit`）；任何档引入 0 轮；`resolve_profile` 的 element→module 归属与蓝图冲突（`docs/architecture/` 尚未存在）；与 FR-008/GOV-003 允许列表定稿冲突。

## B. 实施计划（修订版）

### B.1 门禁前移（关键修订）
**人工实施计划审批 与 test-approved 均置于 S1 之前**。S1 已含实现与测试代码，故流程为：S0 只读基线复跑 → **人工审批实施计划 + test-plan issue 获 `test-approved`** → S1 起才写代码/测试。此前不写任何代码。

### B.2 CLI 执行顺序（明确）
`run_analysis` 新顺序：
1. **先识别与校验模式**：读 `--profile`/`TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE`，非法即报错列出合法值并退出——**早于无终端缺口检查，且零模型调用**。
2. 再检查无终端输入缺项（`unattended_gaps`，`run.py:103-109`/`selections.py:58-71`），但**模式已提供的分析员与轮数视为已满足**，不再作为缺口（旧问答不覆盖模式）。
3. `get_user_selections`（`run.py:111`）：用解析结果的分析员作预选/默认；**模式提供的分析员与轮数直接生效，不被旧问答覆盖**。
4. `_build_run_config`（`run.py:113`）：以解析四项为底，叠加显式来源逐项覆盖。
5. 构造 `TradingAgentsGraph`（`run.py:123`）；6. `message_buffer.init_for_analysis`（`run.py:130`）。

**单一实际配置**：CLI 的执行计划（`build_analyst_execution_plan`）、进度显示（`init_for_analysis`）、分析图（`TradingAgentsGraph`）三处都使用**分解后的同一份实际配置**（同一 resolved 对象），避免各自重算漂移。

### B.3 CLI 与 Python 解析接口（提案）
```python
# profiles.py（或 default_config.py，归属待定）
PROFILE_MAPPING: dict[str, dict] = {...}   # 三档已定值

def resolve_profile(
    profile: str | None,
    *,
    explicit: Mapping[str, Any],           # 仅“显式来源”的 key→值
    defaults: Mapping[str, Any],           # DEFAULT_CONFIG 底值（省略模式用）
) -> ResolvedProfile:                      # dataclass: analysts, max_debate_rounds,
                                           #   max_risk_discuss_rounds, max_tool_rounds,
                                           #   requested_profile, applied_overrides
    ...
```
- **CLI**：`cli/run.py` 组装 `explicit`（来自 `--analysts`、被 set 的 `TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_*`、用户实际回答的深度）与 `defaults=DEFAULT_CONFIG`，调用 `resolve_profile`；结果传给图与进度显示。
- **Python**：`TradingAgentsGraph(profile=..., explicit_overrides=...)` 或 config 内 `profile`；同样调用 `resolve_profile`，省略时走 defaults（零注入）。
- **输入优先级（高→低）**：显式来源（CLI flag / env / API 显式键）逐项覆盖 > 模式映射 > DEFAULT_CONFIG 默认。同 key 多个显式来源时以 CLI flag > env（延续现有“flag/env 显式胜过选择”的精神，具体次序待确认）。

### B.4 追踪
| 需求点 | 步骤 | 文件 |
|---|---|---|
| 三档映射/非法/省略零注入 | S1 | 增 `src/tradingagents/profiles.py`；改 `default_config.py` |
| 四项构造期生效、LLM 前校验 | S2 | `graph/trading_graph.py`（`:78-80` 前校验；`run_settings`；签名） |
| 报告 requested/overrides/resolved | S3 | `graph/trading_graph.py`、`reporting.py`、`report_html.py` |
| CLI 先解析、无终端兼容、三处同配置 | S4 | `cli/main.py`、`cli/selections.py`、`cli/run.py` |
| Python 入口共用规则 | S4 | `main.py`/构造默认（省略=零注入） |

### B.5 改动范围
拟改：`default_config.py`、`graph/trading_graph.py`、`reporting.py`、`report_html.py`、`cli/run.py`、`cli/selections.py`、`cli/main.py`。拟增：`src/tradingagents/profiles.py`、`test/offline/test_profiles.py`、CLI 测试用例（并入 `test_cli_headless.py`/新 `test_cli_profile.py`）。
**不碰**：`.opencode/rules.md`、`.agent/rules.md`、`.env*`、`LICENSE`、CHANGELOG 已发布段、`.github/**`、`llm_clients/**`、`dataflows/**` 安全路径、`test/live/**`；`requirements.yaml` 仅在团队要求时更新且需过 schema gate。

### B.6 可独立验证顺序
S0 基线复跑（只读，对照 03e7d48 的 1407/6/1/24/100）→ **[审批门]** → S1 纯函数单测（映射/非法/省略/覆盖，含 `deep`+辩论=1→风控仍 3；省略签名不变）→ S2 图构造接线 + LLM 前拒绝 → S3 报告三处字段 → S4 CLI/Python（先解析、无终端兼容、三处同配置、checkpoint 区分）→ S5 `ruff check .` + 全量 `pytest -q` 零回归。

## C. 可直接粘贴的 GitHub Issue 正文

```markdown
# test-plan(FR-001): three analysis profiles (quick/standard/deep)

**Requirement:** REQ-TA-FR-001 (must, implement) · relates REQ-TA-INV-003
**Scope:** FR-001 only. All tests deterministic, network-free, under `test/offline/`, each citing `REQ-TA-FR-001` (and `REQ-TA-INV-003` for tool-round cap).
**Approved mapping** (order: analysts, debate, risk, tool-rounds):
- quick = market, news, 1, 1, 3
- standard = market, social, news, fundamentals, 1, 1, 20
- deep = market, social, news, fundamentals, 3, 3, 20

**Gate:** human implementation-plan approval AND `test-approved` MUST both be in place
BEFORE any S1 code or test is written. This issue tracks that gate.

## Acceptance → Scenario → Expected

| Acceptance | Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| Three profiles applied for real | Build graph per profile; assert `conditional_logic.max_debate_rounds`, `max_risk_discuss_rounds`, `graph_setup.max_tool_rounds`; run scripted model, assert analyst node set | Four settings equal approved mapping per key; participating analysts == list |
| Omitted profile == baseline | No profile; test graph/Python entry and CLI `_build_run_config` separately | Graph/Python: all four analysts, 1/1/20; CLI: analyst checkbox + depth 1/3/5 prompts unchanged; no injection |
| Invalid profile rejected before any LLM call | `profile="turbo"` constructed (mocked `create_tier_client` counter) | `ValueError` naming quick/standard/deep; client-creation count == 0; model `_generate` count == 0 |
| Invalid profile precedence (no terminal) | No-terminal run with invalid `--profile` | Reports legal values FIRST; no model calls |
| Valid profile runs headless when config omitted | No terminal; `--profile quick` only (no analysts/rounds) | Runs to completion; profile provides analysts+rounds; no missing-input error for those keys |
| Explicit per-key override (by source) | Parametrized: deep + explicit debate=1; quick + `--analysts`; standard + explicit tool-rounds; override one/many/all | Only explicit keys take explicit value; others keep profile value; **deep + debate=1 keeps risk=3** |
| Old Q&A does not override profile | Interactive-style selections with profile set | Profile analysts/rounds win over stored/answered defaults |
| Report records requested/overrides/resolved | Run with overrides; read `complete_report.md` header, `.html`, `full_states_log_*.json` | All three carry requested profile, explicit override list, resolved four; differences correct |
| No LLM call (test double) | `ScriptedModel` + monkeypatch `create_tier_client` | Reject/early-exit scenarios: count 0; valid scenarios count > 0 (proves double works) |
| Same resolved config in CLI plan/progress/graph | CLI run with profile | `build_analyst_execution_plan`, `init_for_analysis`, graph all use the identical resolved config |
| Checkpoint: omitted profile preserves v0.7.1 signature | Omitted profile vs baseline; compute `_run_signature` | Signature identical (no profile key, four settings unchanged); old checkpoint resumes |
| Checkpoint: changed analysts/rounds differ | Profile change that changes analysts or rounds | Signature differs; resumes fresh, not stale state |
| 0-round semantics (guard only) | If any deviation yields 0 | First Bull/Aggressive still speaks once, then switches (per ADR); not used by the three profiles |
| Full offline regression | `pytest -q` (-m "not integration"), `ruff check .` | Zero regression vs 03e7d48 (1407 passed/6 skipped/1 deselected/24 warnings/100 subtests); ruff passes |
| Traceability/annotation | Scan new source files | Contains `ADR-TBD-FR001` and `REQ-TA-FR-001` |

## How "no LLM call" is proven
Monkeypatch `trading_graph.create_tier_client` to return a counting `_Client`/`ScriptedModel`
(see `test/offline/test_graph_end_to_end.py:52,127`); an invalid profile raises during
construction, so the counter stays 0.

## How full offline regression is done
Clean env `pytest -q` (default excludes integration via `pyproject.toml:61`) + `ruff check .`;
compare against the 03e7d48 baseline; do not run `test/live/**`; no real API calls.

## Gate checklist
- [ ] Human approves implementation plan (this issue's companion PR description)
- [ ] `test-approved` label applied by a teammate
- [ ] Only then: S1 implementation + tests begin
```

## A. 最终 ADR 决策（定稿要点）

**ADR-ID:** `ADR-TBD-FR001`（正式编号待分配） · **状态:** Proposed

- **映射（已定）**：quick={market,news,1,1,3}；standard={market,social,news,fundamentals,1,1,20}；deep={market,social,news,fundamentals,3,3,20}（分析员、辩论、风控、工具轮）。
- **解析位置**：`graph/trading_graph.py` 构造期经纯函数 `resolve_profile(...)` 解析；`default_config.py` 仅新增可选 `profile` 键作请求来源，不预烘模式值。
- **输入优先级（定稿）**：`--profile` > `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE` > `profile`（Python 参数）>（省略模式时）DEFAULT_CONFIG 默认。同字段以“显式来源 > 模式映射 > 默认”为准。
- **选了模式即跳过旧提问**：分析员与深度（辩论/风控）由模式直接生效，不再走旧的分析员勾选与深度问答。
- **字段级覆盖（定稿）**：`--analysts` 与 `TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_DEBATE_ROUNDS` / `_MAX_RISK_ROUNDS` / `_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS` 按**字段**逐项覆盖；示例 deep+辩论=1 → 辩论 1、风控仍 3、工具轮仍 20。
- **Python 接口（定稿）**：`TradingAgentsGraph(profile=..., explicit_overrides={...})`，`explicit_overrides` 独立传入（不与 DEFAULT_CONFIG 混用）。
- **报告字段（定稿，ADR A.5 形状）**：置于 `run_settings()` 允许列表，新增 `profile_requested` / `profile_overrides` / `profile_resolved`（含四项）并加入 `max_tool_rounds`：
  ```
  profile_requested: "quick"|"standard"|"deep"|null
  profile_overrides: {key: value, ...}       # 仅显式覆盖项
  profile_resolved: {analysts:[...], max_debate_rounds:int,
                     max_risk_discuss_rounds:int, max_tool_rounds:int}
  max_tool_rounds: int
  ```
- **Checkpoint（定稿）**：沿用现有配置摘要（`_run_signature` 对排除 `_NOT_IN_SIGNATURE` 的 config 做 sha256，`trading_graph.py:150-165`），**不额外拼接模式名**。省略模式不写 `profile`、不改四项 → 摘要与 v0.7.1 逐字节一致，签名不变；模式或四项改变 → 摘要变化，区分并重新开始。
- **显式 vs 默认区分**：按来源判定（`explicit_overrides`/flag/env 进入 overlay；DEFAULT_CONFIG 仅作省略模式底值），不比对数值。
- **0 轮语义（记录）**：图先执行第一位 Bull/Aggressive 再判断（`setup.py:166,176`）；本轮三档均 ≥1，不使用 0。

```markdown
|---|---|---|
| Profiles applied for real | Build graph per profile; assert `conditional_logic.max_debate_rounds`, `max_risk_discuss_rounds`, `graph_setup.max_tool_rounds`; run scripted model | Four settings equal mapping per key; participating analysts == list |
| Omitted profile == baseline (original Q&A intact) | No profile; run the real CLI path and `_build_run_config` | Analyst checkbox + depth 1/3/5 prompts behave exactly as v0.7.1; no injection; graph/Python = all four analysts, 1/1/20 |
| CLI resolves profile first | Invalid `--profile turbo`, no terminal | Legal values (quick/standard/deep) reported FIRST; zero model calls |
| `--profile` beats `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE` | Both set, conflicting | `--profile` wins |
| Valid profile, headless, config omitted | No terminal; only `--profile quick` + other required inputs; analysts/rounds omitted | Runs to completion; profile supplies analysts+rounds; NOT reported as missing |
| Required inputs still enforced | No terminal; `--profile quick` but ticker/date/provider/model missing | Those gaps listed; only profile-provided analysts/rounds are auto-satisfied |
| Profile skips old analyst/depth Q&A | Profile set with a terminal | No analyst checkbox, no depth prompt |
| Explicit per-field override (by source) | deep + `TRADINGAGENTS_MAX_DEBATE_ROUNDS=1`; quick + `--analysts`; standard + tool-rounds env | Only that field overridden; others keep profile value; **deep + debate=1 keeps risk=3** |
| Report records requested/overrides/resolved | Run with overrides; read `complete_report.md`, `.html`, `full_states_log_*.json` | All carry `profile_requested`, `profile_overrides`, `profile_resolved` (four), plus `max_tool_rounds` |
| No LLM call (test double) | `ScriptedModel` + monkeypatch `create_tier_client` | Reject/early-exit count 0; valid count > 0 |
| Same resolved config in plan/progress/graph | CLI run with profile | `build_analyst_execution_plan`, `init_for_analysis`, graph use identical resolved config |
| Checkpoint: omitted profile preserves signature (S2) | Omitted profile vs 03e7d48 baseline; compute `_run_signature` | Signature byte-identical to pre-change; old checkpoint resumes |
| Checkpoint: changed analysts/rounds differ (S2) | Profile change altering analysts or rounds; compare signatures | Signature differs; resumes fresh, not stale state |
| Checkpoint: no extra profile string | Inspect `_run_signature` inputs | Signature uses existing config digest only; no extra mode-name concatenation |
| 0-round semantics (guard only) | If any deviation yields 0 | First Bull/Aggressive speaks once, then switches; profiles use none |
| Full offline regression | `pytest -q` (-m "not integration"), `ruff check .` | Zero regression vs 03e7d48 (1407/6/1/24/100); ruff passes |
| Traceability/annotation | Scan new source files | Contains `ADR-TBD-FR001` and `REQ-TA-FR-001` |

## No-LLM proof
Monkeypatch `trading_graph.create_tier_client` to a counting `_Client`/`ScriptedModel`
(see `test/offline/test_graph_end_to_end.py:52,127`); an invalid profile raises during
construction, so the counter stays 0.

## Full offline regression
Clean env `pytest -q` (default excludes integration, `pyproject.toml:61`) + `ruff check .`;
compare against 03e7d48 baseline; no `test/live/**`; no real API calls.

## Gate checklist
- [ ] Human approves implementation plan
- [ ] `test-approved` label applied by a teammate
- [ ] Only then: S1 implementation + tests begin
```

## S3 收尾
- `run_settings()` 用 `ResolvedProfile.resolved_settings()` 补齐 `profile_resolved`（含实际分析员与三项轮数上限），保留全部顶层字段。
- 状态日志测试新增断言：`run_settings["profile_resolved"] == {analysts:[market,social,news,fundamentals], max_debate_rounds:1, max_risk_discuss_rounds:3, max_tool_rounds:20}`。

## S4 改动
- `src/cli/main.py`：新增 `--profile` 选项并透传 `run_analysis(..., profile=profile)`。
- `src/cli/selections.py`：
  - `requested_profile(flags)`：`--profile` > `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE`；非法值抛错列出 `quick, standard, deep`。
  - `unattended_gaps`：有模式时不再要求 `--analysts` 与轮数 env，其余必需输入照常检查。
  - `_prompt_selections`：选模式时用映射名单、跳过分析员与深度提问；`--analysts` 仍可覆盖名单；省略模式保持原流程。
- `src/cli/run.py`：
  - `run_analysis`：**先**校验模式（在缺项检查、提问、创建模型客户端之前，非法即列合法值退出）；图构造传 `profile`，`--analysts` 显式时才作为名单覆盖；执行计划、进度缓冲（`init_for_analysis`）与图都取图解析后的同一份 `selected_analysts`。
  - `_build_run_config(…, profile=…)`：选模式时不把交互深度写进轮数配置，避免与模式冲突；省略模式维持原“深度→两轮数”行为。
- 复用并最小修改三处既有 CLI 测试的假图/假函数签名（新增 `profile` 参数、`selected_analysts` 属性），属 S4 接线的必要适配。

## 测试证据（实际 CLI 入口、离线）
新增 `test/offline/test_cli_profile.py`（17 用例）覆盖：
- `--profile` 胜 `TRADINGAGENTS_PROFILE`；env 模式生效；非法模式列三值。
- 有模式时无终端不把分析员/轮数当缺项，但缺 ticker/date 等仍报缺项；省略模式恢复原缺项要求。
- 选模式跳过分析员/深度提问；`--analysts` 覆盖名单；省略模式保留原提问。
- 选模式不注入陈旧深度；省略模式仍把深度映射到两轮数。
- `run_analysis` 把 `profile` 传给图；进度缓冲与图使用同一解析名单；非法模式在无图创建、无提问、无 LLM 前报错列合法值；合法模式无终端且省略配置可跑通；主命令 `--profile` 透传。

# Transcript — REQ-TA-QR-003 component-boundary enforcement

- **Requirement:** REQ-TA-QR-003 (quality; maintainability and dependency boundaries)
- **Test plan:** #25 (`test-approved`; course gate 1)
- **Branch:** `feat/qr-003-component-boundaries`
- **Agent session:** OpenCode, `CS5351/DeepSeek-V4-Flash`
  (`custom-foundry/CS5351/DeepSeek-V4-Flash`), 2026-11-16
- **AI-use log entry:** `AI-2026-11-16-001` in `docs/ai-use-log.yaml`
- **Workflow:** `Prompt.md` (understand → plan → stop → staged build with a human
  gate at every stage → self-verify → record).

This file records the actual implementation and the human review gates of the
session behind this PR. It is not a fabricated replay: each stage below names the
review that changed it, matching the conversation in which the human redirected
or approved the work.

## Scope

The only product code touched is the offline test layer:

- `test/offline/test_layering.py` — extends the existing vendor-layering guard
  with a structural boundary check over the five components
  (`agents`, `graph`, `dataflows`, `memory`, `llm_clients`).
- `test/offline/test_graph_end_to_end.py` — asserts the scripted LLM double is
  actually exercised by a full graph run (`assert model.calls`).

The structural check detects **statically parseable** cross-component private
imports and alias reads (from the AST): absolute/relative `from … import _x`,
`import a.b._m`, private names taken out of a private module, and `alias._x` /
package-alias `df.config._x` / full-path `a.b.c._x` attribute reads resolved to
their import. It does not evaluate dynamic access (e.g. `getattr`, `importlib`,
values passed around at runtime); those are outside its static reach.

`REQ-TA-QR-003` is a test/verification requirement, so no runtime module was
changed; the baseline (v0.7.1 + src-layout, commit `2438f28`) is extended, not
replaced.

## Stage 1 — component-boundary structural check (iterated over review rounds)

The check walks the five components and flags any statically parseable
cross-component private import or alias read, while allowing public calls and
own-component private use. It was refined across several human review rounds;
each round is a recorded intervention. The test counts below are the *directed*
runs of `test/offline/test_layering.py` in the review environment for that round;
the authoritative full-suite result is the post-PR-#27 Windows run recorded under
Evidence.

1. **First draft.** AST scan: absolute/relative `from ... import _x` and
   `alias._x` reads; failure message names the source file and the crossing
   dependency; new modules auto-scanned via `rglob`. Human review caught that the
   two path-based assertions were not cross-platform and that the relative-import
   base was off by one. → Fixed `_resolve_from`, added `_names_path`.
2. **Second round (Windows directed run: 11 passed / 2 failed).** The two failing
   path assertions were made OS-neutral; alias resolution was made per-file (a
   same-named alias in another file no longer leaks); function-local imports were
   scoped to their function; a nested function's parameter no longer shadowed an
   outer read; `df.config._merge` leftmost-name shadowing was handled; and a plain
   `import a.b.c` followed by `a.b.c._merge` was covered. → 17 tests.
3. **Third round (scope resolution).** Human reported three reproduced cases;
   the fix replaced the static alias map with scope-chain resolution and added
   regressions for function-local import + same-function private read (now
   proves interception, not just public calls), no leak into other functions,
   module-alias reassigned as a local, and an inner closure reading an outer
   parameter.
4. **Fourth round (statement order).** Same-function ordered statements had to
   resolve by read position: import → local assign → read passes; assign → import
   → read fails; read → reassign → read reports only the first. Implemented with
   position-aware binding events.
5. **Fifth round (assignment RHS + private modules).** An assignment's
   right-hand side must resolve against the *previous* binding
   (`cfg = cfg._merge({}, {})` → 1); private-module imports across components
   (public name taken from a private module, and `import a.b._secret`) were added.
6. **Sixth round (AST end position).** The `_AFTER_LINE` sentinel was replaced by
   the owning statement's real end position, fixing multi-line RHS calls and a
   same-line following statement; two regressions added.
7. **Seventh round (`for`/`with` timing).** Targets bind after the header
   expression, before the block: `for cfg in cfg._items():` and
   `with cfg._open() as cfg:` each report only the header private call, while the
   block reads of the new local object pass. A SIM102 in `_header_end` was fixed.

Each round was verified with the *directed* structural test
(`test/offline/test_layering.py` in isolation) and `ruff check .`; the human gated
each transition. The full offline suite was run on the Windows validation
environment after these rounds and PR #27 were synchronised (see Evidence).

## Stage 2 — LLM-double assertion in the full-graph test

`test_a_full_run_reaches_a_logged_decision` kept the `ScriptedModel` in a
variable and added `assert model.calls` after `graph.propagate(...)`, proving the
graph invokes the LLM test double (the fixed market-data double and report
assertions were already present and preserved). Parametrized over both output
modes (`free-text`, `structured`). Reviewed and approved.

## Evidence

Directed checks (run in the review rounds; scoped to the touched files):

- `test/offline/test_layering.py`: 30 tests pass (component boundaries + the
  retained `test_vendor_libraries_are_imported_only_by_the_data_layer`); revised
  incrementally per round (round 2: 17, … through 30 by the last Stage 1 round).
- `test/offline/test_graph_end_to_end.py::test_a_full_run_reaches_a_logged_decision`:
  both `free-text` and `structured` pass (asserting `model.calls` non-empty,
  market-data double called, report produced).
- `ruff check .`: clean on the review environment each round.

Full-suite result (authoritative, Windows validation environment):

- After synchronising PR #27 (FR-005) into the branch and completing Stages 1–2,
  the full offline suite passed: **1483 passed**. Windows `ruff` and both output
  modes were green.
- Traceability: `docs/architecture/trace-matrix.md` (QR-003 section).

## Human decisions recorded

- Approved the plan (S1 approach) and each Stage 1 refinement round before code
  moved on.
- Approved Stage 2 after the Windows ruff + full-suite validation.
- Confirmed `test-plan: #25` stays in the PR body (gate 1); the test-plan issue
  carries the `test-approved` label.

# Plan — REQ-TA-QR-003 (test-plan: #25, `test-approved`)

## 0. Environment / branch reconciliation (read-only findings)

- **Branch:** `feat/qr-003-component-boundaries`, HEAD `2438f28`, identical to `integration` tip (no commits yet; the claim landed on `integration` as PR #24). Worktree's `.git` file points to a Windows path `F:/…`; git works here only with `--git-dir=<main>/.git/worktrees/tradingagents-qr-003 --work-tree=.`. Not a repo defect to fix in this task — just how I invoke git.
- **Baseline commit:** `2438f28` (= `integration`). v0.7.1 + src-layout.
- **Requirement:** REQ-TA-QR-003, `status: implement`, owner #WuYu, priority `should`. Fit criterion: *zero boundary violations among new components; `ruff check .` passes; offline suite passes; LLM/data replaceable by test doubles.*
- **Issue 25 acceptance conditions (all must hold):** (1) public calls pass + own-component private helper passes; (2) cross-component private helper import fails for **absolute and relative** forms, and private attribute read via imported alias fails, with the diagnostic naming source file + crossing dependency; (3) new modules are auto-scanned; documented baseline exception matches exact source→target and does not leak to other modules; (4) offline graph scenario with scripted LLM + fixed market data, asserting both doubles were called and a report is produced; (5) `ruff check .` + `pytest -q` green vs integration baseline.
- **Environment caveat:** this Linux worktree has no `tradingagents`/`ruff` installed (`python` absent, only `python3` 3.14). Executing gates needs `pip install -e ".[dev]"` in the dev env. I flag it now; not a code issue.

## 1. Reusable assets already in the baseline (Issue 25 items 1–4 lean on these)

- `test/offline/test_layering.py` — **the existing boundary check** (AST scan; enforces "only dataflows imports vendor libs"). My work *extends this pattern*, keeping the yfinance rule in place as the issue requires.
- `test/offline/test_graph_end_to_end.py` — **reusable LLM + market test doubles**: `ScriptedModel(BaseChatModel)` (scripted LLM, `bind_tools`, `with_structured_output`, `calls`/`fail_at`) and the `offline` fixture that monkeypatches every `router.VENDOR_METHODS` vendor + `snapshot.load_ohlcv` with fixed DataFrames. This is exactly the item-4 "scripted LLM + fixed market data" harness.
- `test/offline/conftest.py` — `_no_network` autouse guard (module boundary safety for offline), `_isolate_config`.
- `test/offline/test_suite_isolation.py` — network/order-independence guard (supports "offline suite passes").
- `test/offline/test_model_validation.py::DummyLLMClient` — a second LLM-double reference (BaseLLMClient subclass).

## 2. Proposed module boundary rule (the test's contract)

Components = the five packages named in Issue 25: `agents`, `graph`, `dataflows`, `memory`, `llm_clients` (under `src/tradingagents/`).

- **Public call:** `from tradingagents.graph.trading_graph import TradingAgentsGraph`, `from tradingagents.dataflows.router import route_to_vendor`, `from tradingagents.llm_clients.factory import create_llm_client/create_tier_client` — allowed.
- **Same-component private:** code in `graph/` importing `graph/setup.py::_analyst_graph`, or `llm_clients/` importing a sibling `_coerce_max_*` — allowed.
- **Violation:** a name beginning `_` whose defining module is in component A, imported/accessed from component B — whether via `from x import _y` (absolute or relative `from .x import _y`/`from ..x import _y`) or via `module._y` attribute read on an imported alias (`from tradingagents.default_config import DEFAULT_CONFIG` then `default_config._TRADINGAGENTS_HOME`).

## C. How each import form is accepted / rejected (revised, cross-component examples)

The scanner resolves each `_`-prefixed name to its **defining component**. A hit is a violation iff `defining_component != importing_component`. Verdicts:

1. **Absolute import — ACCEPT (public):**
   `from tradingagents.llm_clients.factory import create_tier_client` and
   `from tradingagents.dataflows.router import route_to_vendor` → public names, pass.

2. **Absolute import — REJECT (private, cross-component):**
   planted in a temp `graph` file:
   `from tradingagents.llm_clients.factory import _coerce_max_tokens`
   → scanner fails, message contains the **source file** (the graph file path) and the **dependency** (`tradingagents.llm_clients.factory._coerce_max_tokens`).

3. **Relative import — ACCEPT (own component):**
   inside `graph`: `from .setup import _analyst_graph` → same component, pass.

4. **Relative import — REJECT (cross-component):**
   planted in a temp `graph` file:
   `from ..dataflows.vendors.yahoo.ohlcv import _assert_ohlcv_not_stale`
   → resolves to `dataflows` ≠ `graph` → fails, names source + dependency.

5. **Alias attribute read — ACCEPT (public attr):**
   `from tradingagents.dataflows import router` then `router.route_to_vendor(...)` → public, pass.

6. **Alias attribute read — REJECT (private attr via alias), the example you asked for:**
   planted in a temp `graph` file:
   `from tradingagents.dataflows import router` then `router._merge` (or `router.get_vendor`… no — a real private one) → use `from tradingagents.dataflows import config as cfg` then `cfg._merge(...)`.
   Both sides are in-scope (`graph` reading `dataflows`'s `config._merge`), so it's a genuine five-component crossing. Scanner fails, message names the graph source file and dependency `tradingagents.dataflows.config._merge`.

Acceptance for each negative is identical in shape: **scanner returns a violation whose text includes the source file and the crossing dependency**; each is a separate parametrized case so the four rejection forms (abs/rel/alias-private, plus the public/own-component positives) are independently proven.

## Proposed fixes

**1. Alias resolution (false positives/negatives).**
Current `_alias_map` is **global across files** — an alias `config` bound in file A leaks into file B, causing false positives; and a same-file function parameter named `config` shadows the module alias, causing false positives/negatives. Fix: make resolution **per-file** — build each file's alias table from only its `import` statements, then when checking an `Attribute` read, walk enclosing scopes (`ast.FunctionDef`/`AsyncFunctionDef`/`Lambda`/comprehension targets) and if the name is a local binding (parameter, assignment target, `with as`, for target) treat it as shadowed → not a module alias. Add regression cases. Also add a **package-alias** case `import tradingagents.dataflows as df; df.config._merge` → resolve `df`→`tradingagents.dataflows`, and `df.config` is an attribute-chain → resolve to `tradingagents.dataflows.config`, then `._merge` is the private read.

**2. `__init__.py` relative imports.**
In `_module_parts`, for `__init__.py` I currently pop the last part → parts become the directory (e.g. `["tradingagents","graph"]`). Then `_resolve_from` for `from ..dataflows.config import _merge` with `level=2` computes `parts[:len-2]` = `[:0]` = `[]` → wrong. The relative base must be computed as if the module *is* the package: for `__init__.py`, `from .` = its own package, `from ..` = parent's parent. The fix is to keep the module's full dotted name (including the package name for `__init__`) distinct from the component lookup. I'll resolve relative imports using `module_parts_before_pop` (the real module name; for `__init__` it's the directory dotted path) and use `parts[:-1]` (the containing package) as the level-1 anchor, going up `level-1` more. For `__init__.py`, the module *is* the package, so `from .` = itself.

**3. New-module test cross-platform + SIM102.**
Compare normalized `Path` objects (`.as_posix()` / `PurePath`) rather than OS-specific strings. Fix ruff SIM102 (nested `if` in `_private_import_violations` → collapse with `and`).

Let me implement, carefully handling `__init__` relative resolution.

## What I fixed (only `test/offline/test_layering.py`)

**1. Alias resolution.**
- Replaced the global `_alias_map` with per-file `_file_aliases(tree, parts, is_package)` — an alias bound in file A no longer leaks into file B (fixes the false positive/negative).
- Added `_scope_bindings` + `_shadowing_scopes`: if a name is bound locally by an enclosing function/lambda (parameter, assignment, etc.), an `alias._x` read is treated as a local, not a module-alias read (fixes the parameter-shadowing error).
- Added `_alias_target` to resolve attribute chains, so a **package alias** like `import tradingagents.dataflows as df; df.config._merge` resolves `df → tradingagents.dataflows`, `df.config → tradingagents.dataflows.config`, then `._merge` → violation.
- New regression tests: `test_same_alias_name_in_different_files_is_independent`, `test_alias_shadowed_by_function_parameter_passes`, `test_cross_component_private_read_via_package_alias_fails` (the `df.config._merge` case you asked for).

**2. `__init__.py` relative imports.**
- Split `_module_parts` into `_module_info` returning `(parts, is_package)`. `_resolve_from` now anchors relative imports on the package itself for `__init__.py` (`from .` = self, `from ..` = one level up), instead of wrongly dropping an extra level.
- New test `test_cross_component_private_import_from_package_init_fails`: `graph/__init__.py` doing `from ..dataflows.config import _merge` is now correctly reported.

**3. Cross-platform path compare + SIM102.**
- New `_names_path()` compares separator-normalised, `tradingagents`-anchored suffixes (verified against a Windows-absolute and POSIX message); the new-module test uses it.
- Collapsed nested `if` in `_private_import_violations` into a single `crosses = (...)` boolean → SIM102 fixed.

## Bugs and minimal fix

1. **Two Windows path assertions** (`test_cross_component_private_import_from_package_init_fails` line 351 and `test_same_alias_name_in_different_files_is_independent` line 424-425) use raw `in message` / string compares → fail on `\`. Replace with `_names_path`, and the "b.py not present" check with `not _names_path(v, b_path)`.

2. **Function-local imports leak to whole file.** `_file_aliases` walks the whole tree with `ast.walk`, so an `import ... as x` inside one function is treated as a file-level alias everywhere. Fix: only collect aliases whose `Import`/`ImportFrom` is **module-level** (direct child of `Module` body), i.e. iterate `tree.body` (the module statements) rather than the entire tree — imports nested in functions are scoped to that function and won't be recognised as file aliases (conservative: we won't flag reads of them, matching "only in that function's scope"). Keep module-level alias detection for the normal case.

3. **Inner function params must not shadow outer references.** `_shadowing_scopes` walks all descendants of each function and marks every Attribute under it with that function's bindings — so a nested `def inner(config)` incorrectly shadows reads in the *outer* function. Fix: for each Attribute node, determine the *innermost enclosing* function scope and only use that scope's bindings. I'll compute binding sets by walking the scope chain.

4. **`df.config._merge` — check leftmost `df` for local shadowing.** Currently shadowing check only looks at `value` when it's a `Name` (`config._x`). For `df.config._x`, the base of the outermost attribute is `df` (a Name deep in the chain). Fix: after resolving the alias target, verify the **leftmost name** of the chain isn't locally shadowed.

5. **Plain `import` + full module path private read** (`import tradingagents.dataflows.config` then `tradingagents.dataflows.config._merge(...)`). Currently `import x.y.z` without `as` is not in `_file_aliases`, so `_alias_target` won't resolve `tradingagents.dataflows.config`. Add: for `ast.Import` without asname, bind the **top package name** to itself (`tradingagents -> ["tradingagents"]`) so the chain resolves. Then `tradingagents.dataflows.config._merge` → target `[tradingagents,dataflows,config]` → comp `dataflows`, read `_merge` → violation.

Let me implement. First the alias collection + shadowing rewrite.

## Suggested minimal fix (as implemented, only `test/offline/test_layering.py`)

1. **Two Windows path assertions** → switched `graph/__init__.py` and `graph/a.py` checks to `_names_path(...)`; the "b.py absent" check became `not _names_path(v, b_path)`.
2. **Function-local import scoping** → `_file_aliases` now iterates `tree.body` (module-level statements) instead of `ast.walk`, so an `import ... as x` inside a function is scoped to that function only.
3. **Inner function param must not shadow outer** → replaced the "mark everything under a function" logic with `_parent_map` + `_innermost_scope`: each attribute read uses only its *nearest enclosing* function's bindings.
4. **`df.config._merge` leftmost-name shadowing** → `_leftmost_name` finds the chain's root name (`df`), and the shadowing test applies to that root, so `def f(df): df.config._merge` is treated as local.
5. **Plain `import` + full module path** → `_file_aliases` now records `import a.b.c` (no `as`) as `a -> [a]`, so `tradingagents.dataflows.config._merge` resolves and is flagged.
   - `_scope_bindings` simplified to args only (assignments of a namespace are rare; args are the requirement).

## New regression tests added (kept all prior passing cases + yfinance rule)
- `test_function_local_import_is_scoped_to_that_function` — local import not file-wide.
- `test_inner_function_parameter_does_not_shadow_outer_alias` — nested param doesn't hide outer `config._merge` (still flagged).
- `test_package_alias_shadowed_by_parameter_passes` — `def f(df): df.config._merge` passes.

## Proposed approach: scope-chain name resolution

Instead of a single "innermost function bindings" check, build a **binding table per scope** and resolve a name at its **use site** by walking outward:

- Build a parent map plus, for each scope (module, each function/lambda), the set of names bound **in that scope only**: parameters + assignment/`with`/`for`/import targets written directly in that scope's own body (not recursing into nested functions).
- For a read at a given node, find the chain of enclosing scopes (innermost → module). A name resolves to the **nearest** scope where it is bound:
  - If the binding is a **local assignment/parameter** in an enclosing function → it is a local; not an imported alias.
  - If it is an **import** in that scope → use that import's target, but only if the use site is within that import's scope.
- Module-level imports form the base bindings; a function-local `import ... as x` only shadows within that function.

This handles: (1) function-local import + same-function use → resolves to dataflows → flagged; and doesn't leak to other functions. (2) module alias then local assignment `cfg = <obj>` in a function → nearest binding is the local assignment → not an import → no flag. (3) outer `def f(cfg)` param + inner closure referencing `cfg` → resolves to the outer param binding → local → no flag.

Let me read the current functions to rewrite precisely.

The issue: current `_resolve_name` uses a scope's static binding set, so a name's binding ignores *position* within the function. I need flow-sensitive resolution: within a single function body, find the **last** import/assignment of the name *before* the read (by statement order / source position).

## Approach

For the innermost enclosing function scope (and module), instead of a static name→binding dict, compute the binding **position-aware**:
- Collect all binding events (imports and assignment targets) for the leftmost name within that scope body, each with a source position (`lineno`, `col_offset`).
- For a read at position P, choose the binding event with the **greatest position ≤ P** (the most recent preceding binding). If that event is an import → resolve import target; if assignment/param → local.
- If no preceding event in this scope, fall back to the next enclosing scope (same rule), eventually module.

This gives ordered-statement semantics. Let me read the current resolver.

## How ordered-statement resolution works (implemented)

Within a scope, names are no longer a static set — each binding is an **event with a source position**:

- `_direct_binding_events(scope)` returns `(position, name, binding)` for every import/assignment/parameter bound **directly** in that scope (nested scopes pruned). Positions come from `(lineno, col_offset)`; parameters sit at `(-1,-1)` so they precede the body.
- `_binding_at(events, name, read_pos)` picks the binding with the **greatest position ≤ the read's position** — i.e. the most recent preceding binding.
- `_resolve_name` walks scopes outward; the nearest scope that has *any* preceding binding for the name wins; within that scope, the last one before the read wins.

So in one function: import→assign→read resolves to the local assignment (local); assign→import→read resolves to the import (flagged); read→reassign→read flags only the first (import-bound) read.

### OpenCode reply 1 at 2026-11-16 16:37:55

Two gaps: (1) an assignment's **right-hand side** reads must be checked against the *prior* binding (currently the assignment target shadows before the RHS is read); (2) importing a **private module name** (segment starting with `_`) across components, and importing a **public name from a private module** across components, must both be flagged.

Let me explain the fix, then inspect the relevant code.

## Fix plan

1. **RHS evaluation order:** When a `Name` is `Store`d (assignment target), the binding event's position must sort **after** the reads in its right-hand side at the same line. The current `(lineno, col_offset)` of the target `Name` is *before* or equal to RHS columns only if the target is left of `=`. Python evaluates RHS first, so `cfg = cfg._merge(...)` should resolve the RHS `cfg` to the *previous* binding. Simplest robust fix: place a Store event at a position ordered **after** everything on its line — e.g. `(lineno, +inf-like)`. But that would also affect reads later in the same line after `;`. Cleaner: give assignment-target Store events position `(lineno, col_offset + large)` won't work generally. Instead, handle it precisely: for a `Name(Store)` that is the direct target of an `Assign`/`AnnAssign`, its binding becomes effective **after** the assignment statement, i.e. position = end of the statement (`stmt.end_lineno/end_col_offset` is unreliable). Deterministic approach: use `(lineno, col_offset)` but **treat the read position of an RHS node vs the Store target by comparing node identity**: when resolving, if the read node is inside the same assignment whose target is the Store we're comparing, prefer the previous event.

Simplest correct rule that satisfies the case: record Store bindings at position `(lineno, col_offset)`, but for the **read position** use the read's own `(lineno, col_offset)`; the target `cfg` at column 0 on the same line as `cfg._merge` RHS has col_offset 0 < RHS col, so it would wrongly shadow. To make RHS win correctly **without** breaking later same-line reads, I'll place Store events at the **end of their statement line** conceptually: position `(stmt.lineno, +∞)` relative to that statement, i.e. use a special large col for assignment targets equal to a big number. Reads on later lines get larger lineno and still work. Reads on the *same line after the assignment* via `;` are rare; acceptable, but to be safe I'll key on the enclosing statement's line and put the Store after all reads on that line.

I'll implement: Store `Name` binding position = `(node.lineno, LARGE)` where LARGE is a big constant (e.g. 10**9). Param events stay `(-1,-1)`; import events keep their `(lineno, col_offset)` (imports bind after the statement, but a read can't be on the same line before an import in practice; keeping import col is fine). This makes `cfg = cfg._merge(...)` resolve RHS to the prior binding.

2. **Private module imports:** Extend `_private_import_violations`:
   - `from <cross-component module path> import X`: already flags private `X`.
   - New: if any segment of the **target module path itself** starts with `_` (a private module) and crosses components → flag (case: `from tradingagents.dataflows._internal import public_name`).
   - New: `import tradingagents.dataflows._internal` / `from tradingagents.dataflows import _internal` where the *imported module* is private and crosses components → flag.

## Fixes explained (only `test/offline/test_layering.py`)

**1. Assignment RHS resolves before the target binds.**
Python evaluates the right-hand side before assigning. Previously an assignment target's `Store` event sat at its own column, so on `cfg = cfg._merge(...)` the target `cfg` (col 0) appeared to precede the RHS read and wrongly shadowed it. Now Store events are placed at `(lineno, _AFTER_LINE)` (`_AFTER_LINE = 1<<30`), i.e. **after every read on the same source line**, so the RHS still sees the previous binding (the dataflows import) and is flagged. Reads on later lines still resolve to the new (local) binding by line number.

**2. Private module imports across components.**
Rewrote `_private_import_violations` to:
- Handle plain `import a.b.c` too (previously only `ImportFrom`).
- Flag `from <other-component private module> import <public name>` — any import from a `_`-prefixed module segment crosses the boundary even if the taken name is public.
- Flag `import <other-component module path containing a _ segment>`.
- Keep the existing "private name from a cross-component module" rule.
- `_is_private_name` / `_private_module_segment` helpers centralise the check; `__dunder__` still excluded. Same-component and public-module imports remain clean.

### OpenCode reply 1 at 2026-11-16 16:46:05

The `_AFTER_LINE` hack has two flaws: (1) a multi-line RHS (`cfg._merge(\n  1,\n  2)`) puts the target on line N but the read on line N+1 — the read's position exceeds `(N, _AFTER_LINE)` only if `N+1 > N`, so it's fine vertically, but the constraint is fragile; (2) a statement *after* the assignment on the **same line** (`cfg = obj(); cfg._merge`) reads at the same `lineno` but `col > target col` yet `< _AFTER_LINE`, so it's wrongly treated as still the import (false positive). The real signal is the assignment's **end position**.

## Approach: use the actual end position of the assignment

Python's AST gives `end_lineno`/`end_col_offset` on statement and expression nodes (3.8+). Instead of a fake `_AFTER_LINE` column, I'll record each assignment-target `Store` event at the **end of its enclosing assignment statement**: when I encounter a `Store` `Name`, I walk up (via a parent map, or track during traversal) to the owning `Assign`/`AnnAssign`/`AugAssign` node and use `(stmt.end_lineno, stmt.end_col_offset)` as the binding position.

This means:
- RHS reads (anywhere inside the statement, even multi-line) have positions `< end`, so they resolve to the previous binding → flagged.
- A following statement on the same line has `col_offset > end_col_offset` → resolves to the new local binding → clean.

But `_direct_binding_events` currently walks `_walk_own_scope` without parent links. I'll build a small parent map for the scope's own nodes, or track each Store's owning assignment by scanning statements. Simplest: iterate the scope's statements; for each assignment statement, add its Store targets at that statement's end position; for `for`/`with`/comprehension targets, use the node's own end. Let me look at the exact code once more.

## How the AST end-position replaces `_AFTER_LINE`

Previously an assignment target's `Store` event was placed at a fake `(lineno, 1<<30)` column. That was wrong for (a) multi-line right-hand sides and (b) a second statement after `;` on the same line.

Now each `Store` target is bound at the **actual end position of its owning statement**:
- `_local_parents(own_nodes)` builds parent links over the scope's own flattened nodes.
- `_owning_statement_end(node, parents)` climbs from the `Store` target to the nearest binding statement (`Assign`, `AnnAssign`, `AugAssign`, `For`/`AsyncFor`, `With`/`AsyncWith`, `comprehension`) and returns its `(end_lineno, end_col_offset)`.
- Import/parameter events keep their own positions; `_AFTER_LINE` and its comment were removed.

Result: any read inside the statement (including a call spanning several lines) has a position `< end`, so it resolves to the **previous** binding; a statement following the assignment on the same line has `col > end_col_offset`, so it resolves to the **new local** binding. This is exact (uses Python's real statement boundaries), not a sentinel.

### OpenCode reply 1 at 2026-11-16 17:33:42

The current fix binds for/with targets at the **statement start**, so the target's private call in the header (`for cfg in cfg._items()` → `cfg._items()`, `with cfg._open() as cfg`) is wrongly suppressed by the new binding. The correct timing: evaluate the iterable/context-manager expression **first** (against the previous binding), then bind the target **before the block**.

## Approach

A for/with target needs a position that is **after** its header expression but **before** the block body — i.e. between the header's end and the body's start. AST gives:
- `for`: `iter` expression node (`node.iter`) with `end_lineno/end_col_offset`.
- `with`: the last `withitem.context_expr` end.

The target should sit at that expression's end position. Then:
- The header private read (`cfg._items()`, `cfg._open()`) has a position ≤ expression end and `< target binding` (which is at expression end, and the read starts before/at it) → resolves to the previous (import) binding → flagged.
- The block body reads are on later lines → resolve to the new local target → clean.

But careful: the read node `cfg._items()` and the binding position both `≤` expression end. To ensure the read is *strictly before* the binding, put the binding at the expression's end **plus** a tiny offset isn't safe. Better: the read's position is `(lineno, col_offset)` of the attribute — which is at/before the expression end. If I place the binding at the expression **end** `(end_lineno, end_col_offset)`, then a read whose `(lineno,col)` ≤ that end will be `<=` and considered "at or before" → resolves to binding, wrong (the read would see itself). I need the binding strictly **after** the header expression but **before** the body.

For `for`: header ends at `iter.end`; body starts at first statement of `body` (later line). Place binding at `iter.end` position but the read `cfg._items()`'s attribute node has `col_offset` ≤ `iter.end_col_offset`. Actually `cfg._items()` spans from its start to `iter.end`; the attribute `cfg._items` starts at `cfg` col which is `< end`. So read `(start_col)` < binding `(end_col)` on same line → read resolves before binding → flagged.

For `with cfg._open() as cfg`: context_expr = `cfg._open()`, its end is before `as cfg`. The read `cfg._open` starts inside context_expr, `col < context_expr.end_col` → resolves before binding. Target `cfg` binds at context_expr end → block body after → local.

So: binding position = the header expression's `end_lineno/end_col_offset`. Since read start col < end col on same line (or earlier line), reads in the header precede the binding. Good. Let me implement.

For `for`: use `node.iter`. For `with`: use `node.items[-1].context_expr` (last item). Let me update `_binding_position`.

### OpenCode reply 2 at 2026-11-16 17:33:48

There's a subtlety: `_binding_at` uses `pos <= read_pos`. If the binding is at the header expression's `end` and a header read starts earlier on the same line, `read_pos (start_col) <= binding (end_col)` is true — that would mean `binding <= read_pos`? No: `binding_at` checks `pos <= read_pos`, i.e. binding_pos ≤ read_pos. If binding is at end_col (large) and read at start_col (small), binding > read → not counted → read sees previous binding. Correct.

## Outcome of Phase 1

  INFRASTRUCTURE_ERROR with per-case `audit_status` kept separate; redacted
  error tokens; `exit_code_for` mapping (PASS/NOT_APPLICABLE → 0, FAIL → 1,

  `--mode` other than `offline_precheck` is rejected, never reported PASS.
- `test/fixtures/gate4/pm_cases.json` — four fixed cases.
- `test/offline/test_sem_eval_gate4.py` — 35 offline tests, including regressions
  for `expected_reasons: null`, unsupported mode, and pre-existing normal /
