# Gate 4 (Semantic Evaluation Gate) — Read-Only Design Plan

Read: `AGENTS.md`, `Prompt.md`, `REQ-TA-GOV-001`, `DEBT-003`, `prompts/README.md`, `prompts/prompt_v1.0.yaml`, `prompts/prompt_v1.1.yaml`, `ci.yml`, plus grounding in `portfolio_manager.py`, `schemas.py`, `rating.py`, `reporting.py`, `trading_graph.py`, and the existing offline tests/eval harness.

**Status honesty upfront:** `REQ-TA-GOV-001` is `status: candidate`, `priority: wont` (option-ledger row 15). Under `Prompt.md` Step 1, implementing it needs a human decision to promote it to `implement` (or record it as a DEBT-003 slice) **before** code. This plan is design only.

---

## 1. Which files change the runtime prompt/model; how CI evaluates the actual PR diff

**Runtime-prompt / model surfaces (from GOV-001 fit_criterion + source analysis):**

| Surface | File(s) | Why it changes behaviour |
|---|---|---|
| Agent inline prompts | `src/tradingagents/agents/**` (esp. `managers/portfolio_manager.py`, `managers/research_manager.py`, `trader/trader.py`, `analysts/**`) | Prompt f-strings sent to the model |
| Structured schema descriptions | `src/tradingagents/agents/schemas.py` | Field `description`s ship to the model via `with_structured_output` (part of the prompt surface, per `prompt_v1.1.yaml` notes) |
| Reflection prompt | `src/tradingagents/memory/reflection.py` | Model-facing instructions |
| Point-in-time withhold notices | `src/tradingagents/dataflows/date_window.py` | Model-facing notices |
| Versioned snapshots | `prompts/prompt_vX.Y.yaml` | Reviewable diff of the above |
| Model selection (indirectly) | `llm_clients/model_catalog.py`, `llm_clients/factory.py`, provider specs in `openai_client.py` | Changes *which model* runs the prompt — same semantic risk |

- **Trigger set in CI = the fit_criterion paths** (`agents/**`, `agents/schemas.py`, `memory/reflection.py`, `date_window.py`, `prompts/**`). Model-catalog/factory changes are a **gap to decide** (the requirement text says "prompt", not "model client") — see open questions.
- **Evaluating the PR's actual diff:** the job checks out the **PR head SHA** (`github.sha`) and runs the evaluator against that working tree, comparing to the **base SHA** (`github.event.pull_request.base.sha`) *same evaluator, same eval set, same model* — mirroring the existing `ai-use-log-gate`/`secret-scan` pattern (base vs head, `fetch-depth: 0`). This guarantees the measured prompt is the one in the PR, not a snapshot.
- Snapshots (`prompts/prompt_v1.X.yaml`) are **evidence**, not the input to the model; the evaluator reads the **live rendered prompt** from the code path, so a snapshot/code drift is itself detectable (see §5 drift case).

---

## 2. Portfolio-Manager-first: reusable vs. new

**Reusable today (no new model calls required for the deterministic part):**
- **Claim/evidence audit** — `reporting.audit_material_claims()` and `evaluate_claim_acceptance()` are **pure, no I/O, no LLM** (`reporting.py:306,400`). They already emit `ACCEPTED` / `DOWNGRADED` + named reasons (`UNKNOWN_SOURCE`, `UNDATED_MARKET_DATA`, `NO_ANALYST_REFERENCE`, `NO_DATED_OBSERVATION`, `CLAIM_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`, `INVALID_OBSERVATION_DATE`). This is the backbone of quality scoring.
- **Deterministic decision checks** — `rating.validate_final_decision()` (prices/rating → REVIEW), `schemas.decode_strict_json`, `build_decision` (strict NaN/Infinity rejection).
- **Fixtures** — `test/offline/test_claim_acceptance.py::_state` and `test/material_claims.py` already build realistic final-state dicts (ticker, trade_date, analyst reports, debate histories, `material_claims`). These become the **state-level eval cases** with zero model cost.
- **Prompt-capture harness** — `test_structured_agent_prompts.py::_capturing_llm` already mocks structured output and captures the exact rendered PM prompt. Reuse for **prompt-fingerprint / drift** checks.
- **Graph invocation contract** — `TradingAgentsGraph(...).propagate()` and the fake-graph pattern in `test_evaluation_runner.py` (`FakeGraph`) show how to run the graph with a stubbed model.
- **Frozen-input protocol** — `evaluation.py` (`EvaluationCase`, `RunMetrics`) + `docs/evidence/perf001-*` and the recorded apparatus (deepseek-flash, temperature 0, 3 tickers) are the template for a **live** replay tier.

**New fixed cases required (PM-first):**
1. **Deterministic quality set** (no model): hand-structured `PortfolioDecision` objects covering: happy path (ACCEPTED, 3+3 claims, ≥1 analyst cite, ≥1 dated observation); `NO_ANALYST_REFERENCE`; `UNDATED_MARKET_DATA`; `UNKNOWN_SOURCE`; `CLAIM_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`; invalid-price → REVIEW; free-text fallback (no claims → `NOT_ASSESSED`). **Assert the audit verdict + reasons, not just pass/fail.**
2. **Prompt-contract set** (no model): golden rendered-prompt assertions — required sections present (`Rating/Confidence/…`, `Primary analyst evidence`, `NO_EXTERNAL_TOOLS`, `{primary_sources}`), `CLAIM_SOURCE_IDS` injected, language instruction present. Detects accidental prompt edits.
3. **Live semantic set** (model-bearing, `test/live`, opt-in): frozen inputs (AAPL/NVDA/0700.HK at a frozen date, depth-matched to the `perf001` apparatus) → PM only, temperature 0, fixed provider/model → record decision + claim audit + confidence. This is where true "semantic regression" is measured.

**Still needs deciding:** whether v1 covers only the deterministic + prompt-contract tiers (cheap, CI-blocking) and keeps the live tier **manual/recorded** (like `perf001`), or whether the live tier also runs blocking in CI (needs a pinned API key/secret — see §5 gaps and open questions).

---

## 3. Measuring the current baseline first, then human-set thresholds

**Phase 0 — Baseline capture (before any threshold):**
1. Run the **deterministic quality set** against `integration` HEAD → record pass/fail per case as the **floor** (it should be 100% by construction; if not, that's a bug).
2. Run the **prompt-contract set** against HEAD → capture the exact rendered-prompt hashes for `prompt_v1.0` (baseline/v0.7.1) and `prompt_v1.1` (integration) → store as `prompts/` evidence.
3. Run the **live semantic set** N repetitions (e.g. 3, matching `perf001`) on the frozen apparatus → record per-case: `claim_acceptance.status`, `claims_attributed/claims_total`, `confidence`, `final_rating`, `decision_review_reasons`. Archive under `docs/evidence/gate4-<date>/` with `SHA256SUMS.json` + source digests (reuse `perf001` conventions).
4. Publish a **measurement report** (`docs/architecture/evaluation-logbook.md` entry) stating observed distributions, **not** a pass line.

**Phase 1 — Thresholds (human-owned, TBD in registry):**
- Candidate metrics (values stay **TBD**):
  - `claim_acceptance_rate` (share of live runs `ACCEPTED`) — expect near 100% given QR-002 zero-tolerance wording.
  - `claim_attribution_rate` (claims attributed / total; `perf001` observed **18/18**).
  - `prompt_contract_violations` = **0** (hard).
  - `deterministic_set_pass_rate` = **100%** (hard; these are invariants).
  - `rating_review_rate` (REVIEW share) — regression signal.
- **Rule:** thresholds are recorded in a machine-readable `tools/sem-eval/thresholds.yaml` (all values `TBD` until the team signs off), and the registry's GOV-001 `fit_criterion` keeps "TBD until DEBT-003 fixed set" language. The gate must **name the failed threshold** (fit_criterion requirement).
- Follow `AGENTS.md §8` / `Prompt.md` hard rule: **never invent a number** — the gate ships with `TBD` = "advisory/observe only" and cannot block until values are approved.

---

## 4. Script ↔ GitHub Actions split, file list, script interface, failure output

**Division of labour:**
- **Python script (`tools/sem-eval/…`)** = pure measurement + comparison + verdict. Deterministic, offline by default, testable with the existing offline suite. Emits JSON + human summary. Owns threshold comparison and reason naming.
- **GitHub Actions job** = trigger detection (diff paths), checkout (base + head), secret injection for the live tier, running the script, uploading artifacts, mapping non-zero exit → blocking status. **No logic** beyond orchestration, consistent with `ci.yml` style.

**File list (proposed, none created):**
```
tools/sem-eval/
  __init__.py
  cases/quality_cases.yaml         # deterministic PM cases (hand-structured decisions/states)
  cases/prompt_contract.yaml       # golden rendered-prompt assertions
  cases/live_inputs.json           # frozen ticker/date inputs for the live tier
  thresholds.yaml                  # TBD values, human-owned
  fetch_prompt.py                  # render live PM prompt from a checkout (reuses _capturing_llm idea) + hash
  run_quality.py                   # run deterministic set via audit_material_claims/validate_final_decision
  run_contract.py                  # prompt-contract + drift (head vs base hash)
  run_live.py                      # optional live PM replay (pytest integration semantics, injected creds)
  report.py                        # aggregate -> verdict JSON + summary + exit code
  README.md
.github/workflows/semantic-eval.yml  # new job (or extend ci.yml); PR + push, path-filtered
test/offline/test_sem_eval_*.py      # unit tests for each script (offline, no network)
docs/architecture/GOV-001-trace.md   # trace + threshold rationale + baseline report ref
docs/ai-use-log.yaml                 # entry for the implementing session
```

**Script interface (stable contract):**
```
python -m tools.sem-eval.report \
  --base <base_sha_or_ref> --head <head_sha_or_ref> \
  --tier {quality|contract|live|all} \
  [--live --provider deepseek --model <pinned> --repetitions 3] \
  --out <dir> [--thresholds tools/sem-eval/thresholds.yaml]
```
Exit codes: `0` all pass (or all TBD/observe), `1` a fixed threshold failed, `2` harness error (fail closed), `3` skipped (no trigger — set by the workflow, not the script).

**Failure output example (contract):**
```
::error file=src/tradingagents/agents/managers/portfolio_manager.py::[sem-eval] FAILED threshold 'claim_acceptance_rate': observed 0.83 < required 1.00 (TBD-approved 2026-..-..)
sem-eval summary (head 1a2b3c4 vs base 9f8e7d6), tier=all
  deterministic_quality : PASS (8/8)   threshold=1.00 [TBD]
  prompt_contract       : FAIL (2 violations)   threshold=0 [TBD]
    - missing section 'Primary analyst evidence' in rendered PM prompt
    - source_id vocabulary drifted: head drops 'investment_plan'
  live_semantic         : DOWNGRADED 5/6 ACCEPTED (observed claim_attribution 16/18)
    failing case: NVDA@2026-11-13 reasons=[UNDATED_MARKET_DATA]
  verdict: BLOCK (1 hard threshold failed; failing threshold named above)
  artifacts: sem-eval-report.json, head-prompt.sha256, base-prompt.sha256
```
"Failing threshold is named" satisfies the fit_criterion explicitly.

---

## 5. Acceptance criteria, negative cases, skip, and first-version gaps

**Positive (must pass):**
- **Normal change passes:** a benign prompt tweak (e.g. wording in `portfolio_manager.py`) that keeps the contract and audit rates → contract/quality pass, live tier ≥ baseline-or-approved threshold → job green.

**Negative (must block):**
- **Regression blocked:** a change that (a) drops a required prompt section, (b) drops an analyst `source_id` from the vocabulary, (c) makes the model stop emitting `observation_date` for market claims (live → `UNDATED_MARKET_DATA`), or (d) adds `NO_ANALYST_REFERENCE` downgrades — any of these causes a named-threshold failure and **exit 1 → branch protection blocks merge**. Verified by a **deliberate-regression test fixture** in `test/offline/test_sem_eval_*.py` (fault-injected case must return exit 1).

**Skip (must not false-trigger):**
- **Docs-only change skips:** diff touching none of the trigger paths (`agents/**`, `schemas.py`, `reflection.py`, `date_window.py`, `prompts/**`) → workflow path-filter means job **does not run** (not a green that implies evaluation). Add an explicit `docs/-only` test asserting the trigger matcher returns "skip".

**First-version scope gaps (explicit):**
1. **Only PM covered** — analyst/trader/research/risk prompts, reflection, and `date_window` withhold notices are snapshotted/triggered but **not semantically evaluated** (README scope already flags this).
2. **Model-client changes** (`model_catalog.py`, `factory.py`, provider specs) are **not** in the trigger set — a model swap without a prompt edit could ship unmeasured. Decide whether to add.
3. **Live tier credentials/cost in CI** — needs a pinned key or stays manual/recorded; blocking vs advisory must be decided.
4. **Thresholds all `TBD`** — first version is observe/advisory; blocking requires human sign-off (cannot invent numbers).
5. **Frozen inputs drift** — live cases tie to a market-data window; replayability depends on frozen cached data (`perf001` precedent) — scope of "frozen" TBD.
6. **REQ-TA-GOV-001 status** — still `candidate/wont`; must be promoted (or DEBT-003 slice agreed) before implementation.
7. **Snapshot-vs-code drift** is detectable only if `fetch_prompt.py` is run; not yet wired to fail on drift.

---

2. **Tier selection for v1:** ship **deterministic + prompt-contract only** (cheap, blocking) with live tier manual — or include live in CI from day one?
3. **Live tier in CI:** do you authorise a pinned provider key/secret for CI (which provider/model?), or keep the live replay **manual/archived** like `perf001`?
4. **Model-client paths:** should `llm_clients/model_catalog.py` / `factory.py` / provider specs also **trigger** the gate (extending the fit_criterion beyond "prompt")?
5. **Threshold ownership:** confirm `tools/sem-eval/thresholds.yaml` as the home for TBD→approved values, and that the gate stays **advisory** until you sign them.
6. **Initial live cases:** are AAPL/NVDA/0700.HK @ 2026-11-13 (the `perf001` apparatus) the accepted frozen set, or do you want PM-specific adversarial cases first?
7. **Branch/merge gating:** add `semantic-eval` to `main` branch protection required checks now (advisory-tolerant) or only after thresholds are set?

# Gate 4 — Portfolio Manager First Version (read-only plan)

Scope is reduced to **PM decision prompt only**. It runs the **actual rendered service prompt** against **fixed analyst reports** using a **candidate model**, and scores the output with the **existing deterministic claim/evidence audit** (`reporting.audit_material_claims` / `evaluate_claim_acceptance`). No new scoring logic. Thresholds stay **TBD**.

Two distinct layers, kept strictly separate:
- **Offline pre-check** — deterministic, no model, runs in the normal suite (`pytest -q`). Proves the harness and prompt contract are sound.
- **True CI merge gate** — model-bearing replay on frozen inputs; only this one blocks merge.

---

## 1. Fixed inputs + actual prompt + candidate model

**Reuse (no new code):**
- **Actual rendered prompt:** build it by calling `create_portfolio_manager(llm)` with a capturing LLM (pattern from `test_structured_agent_prompts.py::_capturing_llm`, `structured.invoke` side-effect records the prompt) and a **synthetic state** built exactly like `test_claim_acceptance.py::_state` (`company_of_interest`, `trade_date`, `market_report`, `sentiment_report`, `news_report`, `fundamentals_report`, `investment_debate_state`, `trader_investment_plan`, `investment_plan`, `risk_debate_state`). This exercises the real f-string in `portfolio_manager.py:213` plus `primary_analyst_evidence()` and `report_or_absent()` — i.e. the **prompt as it actually runs**, not the `prompts/*.yaml` snapshot.
- **Evidence audit:** feed the model's `PortfolioDecision` back through `build_decision` → `audit_material_claims` → `evaluate_claim_acceptance`. Same code the production run uses; zero duplication.
- **Candidate model:** resolve through the existing `llm_clients` factory/catalog, not hard-coded. Pin provider+model in the case file.

**Fixed analyst reports (the "eval set"):** store as fixtures under a new `tools/sem-eval/cases/` — a handful of hand-authored, versioned final-state dicts (e.g. clearly bullish, clearly bearish, ambiguous, one missing analyst report to exercise `report_or_absent`). These are **frozen** inputs so every model/prompt run is comparable.

---

## 2. Offline pre-check vs. true CI merge gate

| | **Offline pre-check** | **True CI merge gate** |
|---|---|---|
| Runs in | `pytest -q` (normal suite), every push | dedicated `semantic-eval` job, PR only |
| Model calls | none | candidate model, frozen inputs |
| What it checks | prompt renders + required sections present (`Rating/Confidence/Primary analyst evidence/NO_EXTERNAL_TOOLS/{primary_sources}`), `CLAIM_SOURCE_IDS` injected, audit runs on a hand-built decision, snapshot-vs-code drift (hash `prompts/*.yaml` vs live render) | run candidate model on fixed reports, then `evaluate_claim_acceptance` → per-case `status`, `claims_attributed/total`, `confidence`, `final_rating`, `decision_review_reasons` |
| Blocks? | **No** — catches harness rot early; part of gate 5 regression, not gate 4 | **Yes** — failing TBD-approved threshold blocks merge |
| Cost | zero | paid per run |

The offline pre-check keeps the gate honest when no model is available; the CI gate is the actual semantic evidence. The CI job **must** use the **same fixtures** the offline pre-check validates, so the two never diverge.

---

## 3. Which commit is evaluated

- The job checks out the **PR head SHA** (`github.sha`) with `fetch-depth: 0`, matching `ai-use-log-gate` / `secret-scan` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:99-131, 202-289`).
- The prompt under test is **rendered from that checkout's code**, so the evaluated prompt is the PR's real change.
- Baseline comparison: same fixtures + same candidate model run against **base SHA** (`github.event.pull_request.base.sha`); per-case deltas reported. (For v1, comparison may be **descriptive** only; blocking uses absolute TBD thresholds — see §6.)
- Artifacts uploaded: raw decisions, audit JSON, prompt hashes (head vs base), so a reviewer can see the actual diff effect.

---

## 4. Model / prompt change triggers

**Gate fires when the PR diff touches any of (PM-first subset of GOV-001 `fit_criterion`):**
- `src/tradingagents/agents/managers/portfolio_manager.py`
- `src/tradingagents/agents/schemas.py` (its field descriptions ship to the model)
- `prompts/**`
- **Model selection (proposed addition — needs human approval):** `src/tradingagents/llm_clients/model_catalog.py`, `factory.py`, provider specs in `openai_client.py` — otherwise a model swap ships unmeasured.

Detection is the same `git diff --name-only "$BASE" "$HEAD"` + `grep -E` idiom already used in `ci.yml`. If none match → job does **not** run.

---

## 5. Doc-only PRs skip

- Job is **path-filtered** (or an in-job check that echoes "no PM-prompt change: gate not triggered" and exits 0 without calling a model). A docs-only PR therefore **never invokes a model and never produces a green that implies evaluation** — it simply does not trigger.
- Negative test (offline) asserts the trigger matcher returns "skip" for a docs-only diff.

---

## 6. Test-plan draft (thresholds TBD)

**T1 — Trigger matcher (offline)**
- PM-prompt path → trigger.
- `schemas.py` / `prompts/**` → trigger.
- `docs/**`-only → skip (assert no model call path taken).
- (If approved) model-catalog/factory → trigger.
- *Cites:* REQ-TA-GOV-001.

**T2 — Prompt contract (offline, no model)**
- Rendered PM prompt contains: rating scale, `Confidence`, `Primary analyst evidence`, `NO_EXTERNAL_TOOLS`, `{primary_sources}`/`CLAIM_SOURCE_IDS` injection, language instruction.
- Snapshot drift: live render hash ≠ `prompts/prompt_v1.1.yaml` `prompt` block → fail (names drift).
- *Cites:* REQ-TA-GOV-001, REQ-TA-QR-002.

**T3 — Audit wiring (offline, no model)**
- Hand-built `PortfolioDecision` (ACCEPTED happy path 3+3, ≥1 analyst cite, ≥1 dated) → `evaluate_claim_acceptance` = ACCEPTED.
- Inject `UNKNOWN_SOURCE` / `UNDATED_MARKET_DATA` / `NO_ANALYST_REFERENCE` / `CLAIM_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` → each appears in `reasons`.
- Free-text fallback (no claims) → `NOT_ASSESSED`/REJECTED.
- *Cites:* REQ-TA-FR-005, REQ-TA-QR-002, REQ-TA-INV-005.

**T4 — Live replay (CI gate, model-bearing)**
- For each fixed report case: run candidate model (pinned provider+model, temperature 0, N repetitions) at today's HEAD → audit each decision.
- Record per case: `status`, `claims_attributed/total`, `confidence`, `final_rating`, `decision_review_reasons`, `prompt_hash`.
- Compare to `thresholds.yaml` (all **TBD** → v1 observe/advisory; cannot block until values approved).
- A failing threshold prints `::error ... FAILED threshold '<name>': observed <x> < required <y>` and exits 1 → **names the failing threshold** (fit_criterion requirement).

**T5 — Failure blocks merge (CI behaviour)**
- Deliberate fault injection (e.g. fixture case authored so a known threshold must fail, or an env toggle `SEM_EVAL_FORCE_FAIL`) → job exit 1.
- Add `semantic-eval` to `main` branch protection required checks; verify a PR with the fault shows a **red required check** and is **not mergeable** (branch protection proof, recorded in the PR/AI-use log).

**T6 — Doc-only skip proof (CI behaviour)**
- Open a docs-only PR → `semantic-eval` absent/neutral; merge proceeds.

**Exit codes (contract):** `0` pass or all-TBD observe, `1` threshold failed (names it), `2` harness error (fail closed), skip = job not triggered.

**Thresholds:** `claim_acceptance_rate`, `claim_attribution_rate`, `prompt_contract_violations=0`, `rating_review_rate` — **all TBD**, stored in a human-owned `thresholds.yaml`, registry `fit_criterion` keeps "TBD until set built" wording.

---

## Pre-implementation confirmations still needed (stop here)

1. **Promote `REQ-TA-GOV-001`** from `candidate/wont` to a tracked DEBT-003 slice before any code.
2. **Candidate model for the gate** — which provider/model id to pin (and whether CI gets a key; otherwise the live tier is manual/archived like `perf001`).
3. **Add model-client paths** to the trigger set, or keep "prompt/schema only"?
4. **Fixed report cases** — accept hand-authored synthetic states (freshness-proof) vs. reuse `perf001` frozen tickers?
5. **Blocking vs advisory** for v1 while thresholds are TBD.

No files written, no paid models called, no Git writes performed.

## Final `result.json` sample (PASS)
```json
{
  "counts": { "ACCEPTED": 1, "DOWNGRADED": 3 },
  "errors": [],
  "exit_code": 0,
  "mismatches": [],
  "mode": "offline_precheck",
  "reason": null,
  "results": [
    { "audit_status": "ACCEPTED", "evaluator_exception": false, "error": null,
      "expected_reasons": [], "expected_status": "ACCEPTED",
      "id": "valid_dated_claim", "matches_expectation": true,
      "observed_reasons": [], "observed_status": "ACCEPTED",
      "claims_total": 2, "claims_attributed": 2, "unsupported_claim_ids": [] },
    { "audit_status": "DOWNGRADED", "id": "empty_claim",
      "expected_reasons": ["UNTRACEABLE_CLAIM"], "observed_reasons": ["UNTRACEABLE_CLAIM"], ... },
    { "audit_status": "DOWNGRADED", "id": "unknown_source",
      "observed_reasons": ["UNKNOWN_SOURCE"], ... },
    { "audit_status": "DOWNGRADED", "id": "undated_market_claim",
      "observed_reasons": ["UNDATED_MARKET_DATA"], ... }
  ],
  "status": "PASS"
}
```

## Status ↔ exit code
| Overall status | When (mode=offline_precheck) | Exit |
|---|---|---|
| `PASS` | every fixed case matches expectation | 0 |
| `NOT_APPLICABLE` | no case set evaluated / gate not triggered (map: 0) | 0 |
| `FAIL` | any case expected vs observed mismatch | 1 |
| `INFRASTRUCTURE_ERROR` | empty case set, env unavailable, evaluator exception, unexpected skip, unreadable case file | 2 |

Per-case `audit_status` (`ACCEPTED`/`DOWNGRADED`/`NOT_ASSESSED`) is separate from the overall `status`. A `PASS` means the offline pre-check matched — **not** that semantic evaluation passed (thresholds remain TBD).
